[Director 指令] [Director 指令] M1 结果极好——NLI 阈值选择偏差彻底排除。

**立即开始以下两个任务，按顺序执行：**

**1. 0C 100-point Fine-Grained Likert（最高优先级）**
比 0B 更高优先级。Dream 审稿明确指出缺少 scale granularity ablation。
- GPT-4o API via proxy
- 对 NLI-filtered pairs（formality + verbosity），用 prompt "Rate the quality of this response on a scale from 0 to 100"
- 单独评分（不是 pairwise），对 pair 两端分别评分
- 计算 paired t-test / Wilcoxon / Cohen's d
- 如果 d→0 且 p NS → 100-point 也有 Blindspot（最强证据）

**2. 0B Debiased Prompt Verbosity（第二优先级）**
确认开始。复用 correction_poc.py 改为 verbosity axis。GPT-4o API。

另外：
- M1 数据 `data/nli_threshold_sweep.json` 和 bootstrap attenuation `data/bootstrap_attenuation.json` 需要同步到本地。请 cat 两个文件内容让我能确认 Paper Agent 用精确数字。
- Llama 下载进度也检查一下。

import asyncio
import json
import logging
import os
import re
import time

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

import statsmodels.api as sm
from statsmodels.genmod.generalized_estimating_equations import GEE
from statsmodels.genmod.families import Binomial
from statsmodels.genmod.cov_struct import Exchangeable
from scipy import stats

N_BOOTSTRAP = 10000

    "You are an expert evaluator of AI assistant responses. "
    "Compare the two responses below and pick the better one."
)
PAIRWISE_USER = """Instruction: {instruction}

Response A:
{response_a}

Response B:
{response_b}

Which response is better in terms of overall quality (helpfulness, accuracy, completeness, coherence)?
Output ONLY "A" or "B"."""

LIKERT_SYSTEM = (
    "You are an expert evaluator of AI assistant responses. "
    "Rate the quality of the following response to the given instruction."
)
LIKERT_USER = """Instruction: {instruction}
Response: {response}

Evaluate the overall quality of this response based on helpfulness, accuracy, completeness, and coherence. Rate on a scale of 1-10, where:
1-2: Completely inadequate
3-4: Poor quality with major issues
5-6: Acceptable but with notable weaknesses
7-8: Good quality with minor issues
9-10: Excellent quality

Provide your rating as a single integer. Output ONLY the number, nothing else."""


def parse_choice(text):
    text = text.strip().upper()
    if text in ("A", "B"):
        return text
    m = re.search(r"\b([AB])\b", text)
    return m.group(1) if m else None


def parse_score(text):
    text = text.strip()
    m = re.search(r"\b(10|[1-9])\b", text)
    return int(m.group(1)) if m else None

scaled**: `pairwise_results_scaled_formality_qwen32b.jsonl` (355 条, 全部 NLI-pass)\n\n## RATE 方法 axis 可行性梯度（核心发现）\n\n| Axis | 改变了什么 | NLI 策略 | Pass rate | 结论 |\n|------|-----------|----------|-----------|------|\n| Formality | 表面 register | Bidirectional min(fwd,bwd) | 88% ✅ | 完全可行 |\n| Verbosity | 信息量 | Direction-aware (D012/D013) | 86% ✅ | 需 NLI-only gate + direction-aware |\n| Register | 话语情境 | NLI-only (D019) | 95% ✅ | NLI-only gate，与 formality 正交 (50%) |\n| Tone | 认知承诺 | Bidirectional | 45% ❌ | 结构性不可行 |\n\n**D023 决策：所有 axis 统一使用 NLI-only gating**（threshold 0.90）。\n\n## NLI Gate 特性（exp-013 发现）\n\nNLI gate (DeBERTa-v3-large, threshold 0.90) 是 **precision-oriented conservative filter**：\n- Precision 98.9%, Recall 91.6%, PABAK=0.82, AC1=0.89\n- Kappa paradox（Feinstein & Cicchetti 1990）：base rate 88:12 → kappa=0.43 低估\n\n## 完整 3×2 Blindspot 矩阵（C1/C2 核心 evidence — LLMBar）\n\n### Blindspot 矩阵（LLMBar — 3 Judges × 2 Axes）\n\n| Judge | Axis | BT P(style) | BT p | Likert p | Tie Rate | Blindspot? |\n|-------|------|-------------|------|----------|----------|------------|\n| GPT-4o | Formality | 63.0% | <0.001 | 0.332 | 62.5% | ✅ |\n| GPT-4o | Verbosity | 76.0% | 0.0001 | 0.403 | 58.1% | ✅ |\n| Qwen3-32B | Formality | 66.5% | <0.0001 | 0.908 | 72.7% | ✅ |\n| Qwen3-32B | Verbosity | 77.3% | <0.0001 | 0.179 | — | ✅ |\n| **Llama-70B** | **Formality** | **69.5%** | **0.0001** | **0.846** | **72.7%** | **✅** |\n| **Llama-70B** | **Verbosity** | **73.5%** | **0.0001** | **0.752** | **61.6%** | **✅** |\n\n**6/6 cells Blindspot confirmed（3 judges × 2 axes）。**\n\n### Llama-70B GEE Decomposition\n\n| Axis | Style OR | Style p | Orig OR | Orig p |\n|------|----------|---------|---------|--------|\n| Formality | 4.81 | <0.0001 | 3.12 | 0.0007 |\n| Verbosity | 8.21 | <0.0001 | 0.38 | 0.0196 |\n\n**Llama formality = mixed (style+orig 双显著)，verbosity = style-dominated (orig 反向显著)**。与 GPT-4o/Qwen3-32B pattern 一致。\n\n### 0.05-0.07 | ✅ | 小效应 → Likert 完全看不到 |\n| LLMBar verbosity | 76-77% | 0.06-0.08 | ✅ | 高 tie rate (58-73%) 掩盖 |\n| MT-Bench formality | 69-71% | 0.15-0.17 | ❌ | 中等效应 → Likert 也能检测但弱 |\n| AlpacaEval verbosity | 84% | **0.375** | ❌ | 强效应 → Likert 也显著 |\n\n**Pattern**: Blindspot 主要出现在 d<0.15 时。d>0.3 时两种方法都能检测，但 BT 仍远更灵敏。\n\n### 🔑 Bootstrap Attenuation Simulation（2026-05-21, Smoking Gun 级证据）\n\n**区分 measurement attenuation vs power gap**: bootstrap 子采样 Likert 数据，不同 n 下 Cohen's d 是否保持 ≈0。\n\n| Combo | n | d_median 范围 | CI 含 0? | 对应 BT | 结论 |\n|-------|---|-------------|----------|---------|------|\n| **Formality × Qwen3-32B** | 355 | -0.019 ~ -0.015 (flat) | ✅ 全含 | 66.5% | **Smoking gun: d≈0 across ALL n** |\n| Verbosity × Qwen3-32B | 86 | 0.153 ~ 0.169 (flat) | ✅ 全含 | 77.3% | d=0.15 < small threshold |\n| Verbosity × GPT-4o | 86 | 0.197 ~ 0.205 (flat) | ✅ 全含 | 76.0% | d=0.20 borderline |\n\n**Formality 是最强证据**: d_median 在 n=50→355 完全平坦在 ~-0.017，CI 从 ±0.3 收窄到 ±0.1。不是 power gap（power gap 下 d 应随 n 收敛到真值），而是 measurement attenuation（真值本身就是 0）。\n\n### 🔑 100-point Fine-Grained Likert（2026-05-21, 直接回应 Dream Reviewer）\n\n**100-point scale (0-100) 两轴均 NS → Blindspot 不是 scale granularity artifact。**\n\n| Axis | BT P(style)/p | 7pt d/p | **100pt d/p** | Tie Rate (100pt) |\n|------|--------------|---------|--------------|-------------------|\n| Formality | 63.0% / <0.001 | 0.07 / 0.332 | **-0.04 / 0.725** | **65.9%** |\n| Verbosity | 76.0% / 0.0001 | 0.08 / 0.403 | **0.17 / 0.117** | **51.2%** |\n\n**关键**: 在 101 个可能值的 scale 上，52-66% pairs 仍得到完全相同分数。Formality 100pt 比 7pt 还差（p=0.73 vs 0.33）。与 bootstrap attenuation d≈0 形成 converging evidence: Blindspot = absolute-vs-relative 范式差异。\n\n### 🔑 NLI Threshold Continuous Curve（2026-05-21, 44/44 全显著）\n\n**BT 概率跨 NLI 阈值 [0.50→0.98] 完全稳定，证明 style bias 检测不依赖特定阈值选择。**\n\n| Combo | BT Range | Max Δpp | p-values | Sig/Total |\n|-------|----------|---------|----------|-----------|\n| **Formality × Qwen3-32B** | 0.648→0.665 | ~2pp | all ≤0.0005 | 11/11 |\n| **Formality × GPT-4o** | 0.614→0.642 | ~3pp | all ≤0.002 | 11/11 |\n| **Verbosity × Qwen3-32B** | 0.765→0.773 | ~1pp | all =0.0001 | 11/11 |\n| **Verbosity × GPT-4o** | 0.752→0.769 | ~2pp | all =0.0001 | 11/11 |\n\n**核心发现**: 即使从 0.50（几乎不过滤）到 0.98（极端严格），BT | ✅ |\n\n**Axis-specific correction resistance**: Verbosity 可部分干预（50.4%），Formality 几乎不可干预（GPT-4o 3.1%）。但**三个组合 debiased 后全部仍然显著** → C4 claim 成立：style bias 比 prompt-level intervention 更深层。\n**GPT-4o verbosity 额外发现**: ~5 pairs 回复 \"Neither\"（拒绝选择），consistent rate 65%。\n\n### AlpacaEval Verbosity × Qwen3-32B — 跨 benchmark verbosity 确认\n\n| Metric | 值 |\n|--------|---|\n| BT P(verbose) | 84.1% style×originality interaction 不显著（GPT-4o p=0.53, Qwen3-32B p=0.32）→ additive model 正确\n\n### 跨模型一致性（论文核心 argument — 3 judges 确认）\n- **Verbosity**: 三模型 Style OR=8-13，Orig OR<1 → **纯 style bias 是 model-independent**\n- **Formality**: 三模型 Style+Orig 均显著，pattern 一致（mixed effect）\n- **Register**: 两模型 Style OR≈1.9 → **几乎相同的边缘 style effect**（Llama 未测 register）\n\n### C3 Axis Gradient\n- **Verbosity = 纯 style bias**（OR=8-13，originality ns 或反向）\n- **Formality = 混合效应**（style + originality 共存，三 judge 均双显著）\n- **Register = originality 主导**（style 边缘）\n\n## Verbosity vs Formality 的方向不对称性差异（关键机制洞察）\n\n**Verbosity = 真正的 style bias**（非 originality bias）：\n- verbose→concise: 80.6% prefer original（verbose）\n- concise→verbose: 90.7% prefer rewrite（verbose）\n- 两个方向都偏好 verbose content → 真正的 verbosity preference\n\n**Formality = 可能混合 originality bias**：\n- formal→casual: 81.4% prefer original (p<0.0001)\n- casual→formal: 50.0% (p=1.0, 无偏好)\n\n**Register = 主要是 originality bias**：\n- decomposition 是正确的 artifact 控制。\n\n### CF vs DR — 排除出论文主叙事\n- CF = 1 次改写, DR = 2 次改写 → rewrite 次数不等导致 polish confound\n- Reviewer #68/#69 独立评估一致：排除出论文\n\n## BT Canonical 数字 (D032)\n\n- **NLI-filtered Axis | BT P(style) | BT p | Likert p | Tie Rate | Blindspot? |\n|-------|------|-------------|------|----------|----------|------------|\n| GPT-4o | Formality | 63.0% | <0.001 | 0.332 | 62.5% | ✅ |\n| GPT-4o | Verbosity | 76.0% | 0.0001 | 0.403 | 58.1% | ✅ |\n| Qwen3-32B | Formality | 66.5% | <0.0001 | 0.908 | 72.7% | ✅ |\n| Qwen3-32B | Verbosity | 77.3% | <0.0001 | 0.179 | — | ✅ |\n| **Llama-70B** | **Formality** | **69.5%** | **0.0001** | **0.846** | **72.7%** | **✅** |\n| **Llama-70B** | **Verbosity** | **73.5%** | **0.0001** | **0.752** | **61.6%** | **✅** |\n\n**6/6 cells Blindspot confirmed（3 judges × 2 axes）。**\n\n### Llama-70B GEE Decomposition\n\n| Axis | Style OR | Style p | Orig OR | Orig p |\n|------|----------|---------|---------|--------|\n| Formality | 4.81 | <0.0001 | 3.12 | 0.0007 |\n| Verbosity | 8.21 | <0.0001 | 0.38 | 0.0196 |\n\n**Llama formality = mixed (style+orig 双显著)，verbosity = style-dominated (orig 反向显著)**。与 GPT-4o/Qwen3-32B pattern 一致。\n\n### Cross-Benchmark Formality Blindspot 矩阵\n\n| | GPT-4o | Qwen3-32B |\n|---|---|---|\n| **LLMBar** | ✅ Blindspot (Likert p=0.332) | ✅ Blindspot (Likert p=0.908) |\n| **AlpacaEval** | ✅ Blindspot (Likert p=0.170) | ✅ Blindspot (Likert p=0.908) |\n| **MT-Bench** | ❌ NOT Blindspot (Likert p=0.016) | ❌ NOT Blindspot (Likert p=0.007) |\n\n**4/6 cells Blindspot**。MT-Bench 的 style bias 足够强连 Likert 都能检测到。\n\n### Blindspot 受 Effect Size 调控（关键 nuance）\n\n| 场景 | BT P(style) | Cohen's d | Blindspot? | 解读 |\n|------|-------------|-----------|------------|------|\n| LLMBar formality | 63-66% | 0.05-0.07 | ✅ | 小效应 → Likert 完全看不到 |\n| LLMBar verbosity | 76-77% | 0.06-0.08 | ✅ | 高 tie rate (58-73%) 掩盖 |\n| MT-Bench formality | 69-71% | 0.15-0.17 | ❌ | 中等效应 → Likert 也能检测但弱 |\n| AlpacaEval verbosity | 84% | **0.375** | ❌ | 强效应 → Likert 也显著 |\n\n**Pattern**: Blindspot 主要出现在 d<0.15 时。d>0.3 时两种方法都能检测，但 BT 仍远更灵敏。\n\n### 🔑 Bootstrap Attenuation Simulation（2026-05-21, Smoking Gun 级证据）\n\n**区分 measurement attenuation vs power gap**: bootstrap 子采样 Likert 数据，不同 n 下 Cohen's d 是否保持 ≈0。\n\n| Combo | n | d_median 范围 | CI 含 0? | 对应 BT | 结论 |\n|-------|---|-------------|----------|---------|------|\n| **Formality × Qwen3-32B** | 355 | -0.019 ~ -0.015 (flat) | ✅ 全含 | 66.5% | **Smoking gun: d≈0 across ALL n** |\n| Verbosity × Qwen3-32B | 86 | 0.153 ~ 0.169 (flat) | ✅ 全含 | 77.3% | d=0.15 < small threshold |\n| Verbosity × GPT-4o | 86 | 0.197 ~ 0.205 (flat) | ✅ 全含 | 76.0% | d=0.20 borderline |\n\n**Formality 是最强证据**: d_median 在 n=50→355 完全平坦在 ~-0.017，CI 从 ±0.3 收窄到 ±0.1。不是 power gap（power gap 下 d 应随 n 收敛到真值），而是 measurement attenuation（真值本身就是 0）。\n\n### 🔑 100-point Fine-Grained Likert（2026-05-21, 直接回应 Dream Reviewer）\n\n**100-point scale (0-100) 两轴均 NS → Blindspot 不是 scale granularity artifact。**\n\n| Axis | BT P(style)/p | 7pt d/p | **100pt d/p** | Tie Rate (100pt) |\n|------|--------------|---------|--------------|-------------------|\n| Formality | 63.0% / <0.001 | 0.07 / 0.332 | **-0.04 / 0.725** | **65.9%** |\n| Verbosity | 76.0% / 0.0001 | 0.08 / 0.403 | **0.17 / 0.117** | **51.2%** |\n\n**关键**: 在 101 个可能值的 scale 上，52-66% pairs 仍得到完全相同分数。Formality 100pt 比 7pt 还差（p=0.73 vs 0.33）。与 bootstrap attenuation d≈0 形成 converging evidence: Blindspot = absolute-vs-relative 范式差异。\n\n### 🔑 NLI Threshold Continuous Curve（2026-05-21, 44/44 全显著）\n\n**BT 概率跨 NLI 阈值 [0.50→0.98] 完全稳定，证明 style bias 检测不依赖特定阈值选择。**\n\n| Combo | BT Range | Max Δpp | p-values | Sig/Total |\n|-------|----------|---------|----------|-----------|\n| **Formality × Qwen3-32B** | 0.648→0.665 | ~2pp | all ≤0.0005 | 11/11 |\n| **Formality × GPT-4o** | 0.614→0.642 | ~3pp | all ≤0.002 | 11/11 |\n| **Verbosity × Qwen3-32B** | 0.765→0.773 | ~1pp | all =0.0001 | 11/11 |\n| **Verbosity × GPT-4o** | 0.752→0.769 | ~2pp | all =0.0001 | 11/11 |\n\n**核心发现**: 即使从 0.50（几乎不过滤）到 0.98（极端严格），BT 概率波动 ≤3pp，p-value 始终 ≤0.002。Verbosity 两个 judge 的 BT 都在 0.75-0.77 范围内，formality 在 0.61-0.66 范围内。这排除了\"NLI 阈值选择偏差\"的 reviewer concern。\n\n### Claude Sonnet 4 — Negative Control (D047)\n\n| Axis | BT P(style) | BT p | Likert p | GEE Style OR | GEE Orig OR |\n|------|-------------|------|----------|--------------|-------------|\n| Formality | 56.2% | 0.091 (NS) | 0.721 | 1.47 (NS) | **6.66*** |\n| Verbosity | 54.1% | 0.284 (NS) | 0.743 | 1.52 (NS) | 0.80 (NS) |\n\n**Claude 没有 style bias**——BT/Likert/GEE style 全部 NS。验证 RATE pipeline 不产生 false positive。\n**Claude formality 有极强 originality bias** (OR=6.66)：偏好原始文本而非改写文本，但不偏好特定风格。\n**Spec 一致性**：3 个 judge 使用完全相同的 system prompt + temperature=0.0。\n\n### Correction PoC — Debiased Prompt Axis-Dependent Resistance（支持 C4）\n\n| Judge | Axis | Original P(style) | Debiased P(style) | Reduction | 仍显著? |\n|-------|------|-------------------|-------------------|-----------|---------|\n| GPT-4o | **Verbosity** | **76.0%** (p=0.0001) | **62.9%** (p=0.0016) | **-13.1pp (50.4%)** | ✅ |\n| Qwen3-32B | Formality | 66.5% (p<0.0001) | 60.8% (p=0.002) | -5.7pp (34.5%) | ✅ |\n| GPT-4o | Formality | 63.0% (p=0.0004) | 62.6% (p<0.0001) | -0.4pp (3.1%) | ✅ |\n\n**Axis-specific correction resistance**: Verbosity 可部分干预（50.4%），Formality 几乎不可干预（GPT-4o 3.1%）。但**三个组合 debiased 后全部仍然显著** → C4 claim 成立：style bias 比 prompt-level intervention 更深层。\n**GPT-4o verbosity 额外发现**: ~5 pairs 回复 \"Neither\"（拒绝选择），consistent rate 65%。\n\n### AlpacaEval Verbosity × Qwen3-32B — 跨 benchmark verbosity 确认\n\n| Metric | 值 |\n|--------|---|\n| BT P(verbose) | 84.1% [77.4%, 90.2%], p=0.0001 |\n| Likert ATE | +0.73, p=0.0003, d=0.375 |\n| GEE Style OR | **28.64** [9.16, 89.56], p<0.0001 |\n| GEE Orig OR | 0.18 [0.06, 0.54], p=0.002 |\n| n_pairs | 82 (NLI≥0.90) |\n\n### GPT-4o Rewriter Independence — 双轴确认 (R3-N2)\n\n| Axis | GPT-4o Rewriter BT | Qwen3-32B Rewriter BT | Δpp | CI Overlap | Independence |\n|------|-------|---------|-----|------------|---|\n| **Formality** | 64.4% [56.1, 72.8] | 66.5% [58.5, 73.9] | -2.1 | ✅ baseline within CI | ✅ |\n| **Verbosity** | 73.5% [65.4, 80.9] | 77.3% [70.3, 84.3] | -3.8 | ✅ CIs overlap | ✅ |\n\n### R3 Pipeline |\n|-------|------|----------|---------|---------|--------|------|\n| Qwen3-32B | **Verbosity** | **13.10** | 2.6e-09 | 0.49 | 0.096 ns | **纯 style bias** |\n| GPT-4o | **Verbosity** | **11.34** | 0.0002 | 4.70 | 5.2e-05 | Style ≈ Orig |\n| GPT-4o | Formality | 2.58 | 0.002 | 2.32 | 0.006 | Style ≈ Orig (both sig) |\n| **Llama-70B** | **Formality** | **4.81** | **<0.0001** | **3.12** | **0.0007** | **Style ≈ Orig (both sig)** |\n| GPT-4o | Register | 1.88 | 0.027 | 2.21 | 0.005 | Orig > Style |\n| Qwen3-32B | Register | 1.91 | 0.046 | 5.72 | 8.5e-08 | Orig >> Style |\n\n**D041 决策**: 论文全文统一使用 `gee_diagnostics.py` 的 NLI≥0.90 过滤数字。\n\n### 🔑 Length Confound 验证（Dream W10 — D040 已通过）\n\n**结论：Verbosity style OR 在控制 text length 后完全存活，length bias 假说不成立。**\n\n| Judge | Model | Style OR | Style p | Length OR | Length p |\n|-------|-------|----------|---------|-----------|----------|\n| Qwen3-32B | A: No length | 13.10 | 2.6e-09 | — | — |\n| Qwen3-32B | B: + length_ratio | 13.10 | **Correlation Structure**: Exchangeable vs Independence |ΔQIC| < 0.001, Max|ΔCoef| < 0.02（所有 6 cells）→ 实质等价\n- **α 范围**: [-0.25, +0.11]，within-pair correlation 弱\n- **Formality Interaction**: style×originality interaction 不显著（GPT-4o p=0.53, Qwen3-32B p=0.32）→ additive model 正确\n\n### 跨模型一致性（论文核心 argument — 3 judges 确认）\n- **Verbosity**: 三模型 Style OR=8-13，Orig OR<1 → **纯 style bias 是 model-independent**\n- **Formality**: 三模型 Style+Orig 均显著，pattern 一致（mixed effect）\n- **Register**: 两模型 Style OR≈1.9 → **几乎相同的边缘 style effect**（Llama 未测 register）\n\n### C3 Axis Gradient\n- **Verbosity = 纯 style bias**（OR=8-13，originality ns 或反向）\n- **Formality = 混合效应**（style + originality 共存，三 judge 均双显著）\n- **Register = originality 主导**（style 边缘）\n\n## Verbosity vs Formality 的方向不对称性差异（关键机制洞察）\n\n**Verbosity = 真正的 style bias**（非 originality bias）：\n- verbose→concise: 80.6% prefer original（verbose）\n- concise→verbose: 90.7% prefer rewrite（verbose）\n- 两个方向都偏好 verbose content → 真正的 verbosity preference\n\n**Formality = 可能混合 originality bias**：\n- formal→casual: 81.4% prefer original (p<0.0001)\n- casual→formal: 50.0% (p=1.0, 无偏好)\n\n**Register = 主要是 originality bias**：\n- academic→conv: 89.3% prefer original\n- conv→academic: 68.8% prefer original\n\n## 🔑 Double-Rewrite Placebo + CF vs DR 发现（2026-05-21）\n\n### ⚠️ 概念区分（Director/Reviewer 纠正）\n- **Rewriting artifact** = judge 真实偏好 LLM-polished text → 任何测量 format 都会体现\n- **Format amplification** = pairwise FORMAT 夸大差异 → Comparative Trap 的论点\n- Placebo 检测到的是 **rewriting artifact**，**NOT format amplification**\n- **MF1 三层防御**针对的是 format amplification，完全不受 placebo 结果影响\n\n### Placebo BT 修正（choix.ilsr_pairwise + ALL pairs）\n- Qwen3-32B formality: P(orig)=0.377 [0.303,0.451] p=0.0006\n- Qwen3-32B verbosity: P(orig)=0.082 [0.035,0.140] p<0.0001\n- GPT-4o formality: P(orig)=0.418 [0.342,0.500] p=0.037\n- GPT-4o verbosity: P(orig)=0.167 [0.084,0.262] p<0.0001\n- **结论**: Judges 系统性偏好 LLM-rewritten text。GEE decomposition 是正确的 artifact 控制。\n\n### CF vs DR — 排除出论文主叙事\n- CF = 1 次改写, DR = 2 次改写 → rewrite 次数不等导致 polish confound\n- Reviewer #68/#69 独立评估一致：排除出论文\n\n## BT Canonical 数字 (D032)\n\n- **NLI-filtered (primary)**: P(formal)=63.0%, CI [55.8%, 70.3%], p<0.001, 88 pairs/173 comp\n- **All-data (robustness)**: P(formal)=60.9%, CI [53.8%, 67.8%], p=0.001, 100 pairs/197 comp\n\n## Power Curve Canonical p=0.606, tie=0.625 | 462 |\n| Likert ATE | d=0.073 (raw) | 1,473 |\n\n## Canonical 数字源（D041 决策）\n- **GEE 6-cell 表**: `csd_pipeline/gee_diagnostics.py`（NLI≥0.90 过滤，88 pairs formality）\n- **Length confound**: `csd_pipeline/verbosity_length_confound.py`\n- 废弃: `csd_pipeline/gpt4o_analysis.py` 的 GEE 数字（exp014, 98 pairs，含 NLI<0.90）\n\n## 分析脚本路径\n- **Canonical GEE (所有 6 cells)**: """Correction PoC: debiased prompt pairwise scoring + BT analysis.

Runs position-controlled dual-trial pairwise scoring with a debiased prompt
on the 88 NLI-pass formality pairs, for both Qwen3-32B and GPT-4o judges.
Then compares with original (standard prompt) results.
"""

import json
import re
import sys
import time

import choix
import numpy as np
from openai import AsyncOpenAI

GPT4O_MAX_TOKENS = 16

DEBIASED_SYSTEM = (
    "You are an expert evaluator of AI assistant responses. "
    "Compare the two responses below and pick the better one."
)

DEBIASED_USER_TEMPLATE = """Instruction: {instruction}

Response A:
{response_a}

Response B:
{response_b}

IMPORTANT: Do NOT consider writing style differences (such as formality, verbosity, or register) when making your judgment. Focus exclusively on the substantive content quality, accuracy, and helpfulness of each response. Style variations should not influence your choice.

Which response is better in terms of overall quality (helpfulness, accuracy, completeness, coherence)?
Output ONLY "A" or "B"."""

{
  "command": "ssh -o ConnectTimeout=10 -p 29275 root@connect.westb.seetacloud.com \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 -c \\\"\nimport json, numpy as np, choix\n\ndata = [json.loads(l) for l in open('/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/pairwise_results_alpacaeval_verbosity_qwen32b.jsonl') if l.strip()]\nnli = [d for d in data if d.get('nli_cf',0) >= 0.90]\n\nVERBOSE, CONCISE = 0, 1\npair_groups = []\nfor rec in nli:\n    pcomps = []\n    t1 = rec.get('trial1_prefers_verbose')\n    if t1 is not None:\n        pcomps.append((VERBOSE, CONCISE) if t1 else (CONCISE, VERBOSE))\n    t2 = rec.get('trial2_prefers_verbose')\n    if t2 is not None:\n        pcomps.append((VERBOSE, CONCISE) if t2 else (CONCISE, VERBOSE))\n    if pcomps:\n        pair_groups.append(pcomps)\n\nall_comps = [c for g in pair_groups for c in g]\nparams = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, all_comps, alpha=0.01)\np_verbose = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-(params[VERBOSE] - params[CONCISE])))\n\nrng = np.random.RandomState(42)\nn_pairs = len(pair_groups)\nboot_p = []\nfor _ in range(10000):\n    idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n    bc = [c for i in idx for c in pair_groups[i]]\n    try:\n        bp = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, bc, alpha=0.01)\n        boot_p.append(1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-(bp[VERBOSE] - bp[CONCISE]))))\n    except: pass\nboot_p = np.array(boot_p)\nci_lo, ci_hi = np.percentile(boot_p, [2.5, 97.5])\nn_below = np.sum(boot_p < 0.5)\npval = 2 * min(n_below, len(boot_p) - n_below) / len(boot_p)\npval = max(pval, 1.0 / 10000)\n\nconsistent = [d for d in nli if d.get('consistent')]\nvw = sum(1 for d in consistent if d.get('verbose_wins'))\n\nprint(f'n_pairs={n_pairs}, n_comps={len(all_comps)}')\nprint(f'BT P(verbose)={p_verbose*100:.1f}% [{ci_lo*100:.1f}, {ci_hi*100:.1f}], p={pval}')\nprint(f'Consistent: {len(consistent)}/{len(nli)}, Verbose wins: {vw}/{len(consistent)}')\n\\\"\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Run BT analysis on existing AlpacaEval verbosity data",
  "timeout": "60000"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/tmp/llama70b_debiased.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Llama-70B debiased prompt pairwise scoring for formality + verbosity.\n\nRuns position-controlled dual-trial pairwise scoring with axis-specific\ndebiased prompts on Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct (vLLM port 8003).\nComputes BT analysis and compares with baseline to quantify correction resistance.\n\nFormality debiased prompt matches correction_poc.py (general style).\nVerbosity debiased prompt matches debiased_verbosity_gpt4o.py (verbosity-specific).\n\"\"\"\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport re\nimport time\n\nimport choix\nimport numpy as np\nfrom openai import AsyncOpenAI\n\nBASE_URL = \"http://localhost:8003/v1\"\nAPI_KEY = \"[REDACTED]\"\nNLI_THRESHOLD = 0.90\nN_BOOT = 10000\nSEED = 42\nMODEL = None\n\nDEBIASED_SYSTEM = (\n    \"You are an expert evaluator of AI assistant responses. \"\n    \"Compare the two responses below and pick the better one.\"\n)\n\nDEBIASED_FORMALITY_USER = \"\"\"Instruction: {instruction}\n\nResponse A:\n{response_a}\n\nResponse B:\n{response_b}\n\nIMPORTANT: Do NOT consider writing style differences (such as formality, verbosity, or register) when making your judgment. Focus exclusively on the substantive content quality, accuracy, and helpfulness of each response. Style variations should not influence your choice.\n\nWhich response is better in terms of overall quality (helpfulness, accuracy, completeness, coherence)?\nOutput ONLY \"A\" or \"B\".\"\"\"\n\nDEBIASED_VERBOSITY_USER = \"\"\"Instruction: {instruction}\n\nResponse A:\n{response_a}\n\nResponse B:\n{response_b}\n\nIMPORTANT: Do NOT consider differences in length, level of detail, or verbosity when making your judgment. A longer, more detailed response is not inherently better than a concise one, and vice versa. Focus exclusively on the substantive content quality, accuracy, and helpfulness of each response.\n\nWhich response is better in terms of overall quality (helpfulness, accuracy, completeness, coherence)?\nOutput ONLY \"A\" or \"B\".\"\"\"\n\nAXIS_CONFIG = {\n    \"formality\": {\n        \"data_path\": \"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/verified_32b.jsonl\",\n        \"orig_field\": \"original_formality\",\n        \"pole_a\": \"formal\",\n        \"pole_b\": \"casual\",\n        \"pref_key\": \"prefers_formal\",\n        \"user_template\": DEBIASED_FORMALITY_USER,\n        \"baseline_bt\": 69.5,\n    },\n    \"verbosity\": {\n        \"data_path\": \"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/verified_verbosity.jsonl\",\n        \"orig_field\": \"original_verbosity\",\n        \"pole_a\": \"verbose\",\n        \"pole_b\": \"concise\",\n        \"pref_key\": \"prefers_verbose\",\n        \"user_template\": DEBIASED_VERBOSITY_USER,\n        \"baseline_bt\": 73.5,\n    },\n}\n\n\ndef parse_choice(text):\n    text = text.strip().upper()\n    if text in (\"A\", \"B\"):\n        return text\n    m = re.search(r\"\\b([AB])\\b\", text)\n    return m.group(1) if m else None\n\n\nclass RateLimiter:\n    def __init__(self, interval=0.05):\n        self._interval = interval\n        self._last = 0.0\n        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()\n\n    async def acquire(self):\n        async with self._lock:\n            now = time.monotonic()\n            wait = self._interval - (now - self._last)\n            if wait > 0:\n                await asyncio.sleep(wait)\n            self._last = time.monotonic()\n\n\nasync def call_llm(client, messages, limiter, max_retries=3):\n    for attempt in range(max_retries):\n        await limiter.acquire()\n        try:\n            resp = await client.chat.completions.create(\n                model=MODEL, messages=messages, temperature=0.0, max_tokens=16,\n            )\n            return resp.choices[0].message.content.strip()\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  [ERR] attempt {attempt+1}: {e}\", flush=True)\n            if attempt < max_retries - 1:\n                await asyncio.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))\n    return None\n\n\nasync def score_record(client, rec, axis, semaphore, limiter, progress):\n    async with semaphore:\n        cfg = AXIS_CONFIG[axis]\n        instruction = rec[\"instruction\"]\n        orig = rec[cfg[\"orig_field\"]]\n\n        if orig == cfg[\"pole_a\"]:\n            pole_a_text, pole_b_text = rec[\"original_text\"], rec[\"counterfactual_text\"]\n        else:\n            pole_a_text, pole_b_text = rec[\"counterfactual_text\"], rec[\"original_text\"]\n\n        # Trial 1: A=pole_a, B=pole_b\n        msgs1 = [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": DEBIASED_SYSTEM},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": cfg[\"user_template\"].format(\n                instruction=instruction, response_a=pole_a_text, response_b=pole_b_text)},\n        ]\n        t1_raw = await call_llm(client, msgs1, limiter)\n        t1_choice = parse_choice(t1_raw) if t1_raw else None\n\n        # Trial 2: A=pole_b, B=pole_a\n        msgs2 = [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": DEBIASED_SYSTEM},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": cfg[\"user_template\"].format(\n                instruction=instruction, response_a=pole_b_text, response_b=pole_a_text)},\n        ]\n        t2_raw = await call_llm(client, msgs2, limiter)\n        t2_choice = parse_choice(t2_raw) if t2_raw else None\n\n        t1_pref = (t1_choice == \"A\") if t1_choice else None\n        t2_pref = (t2_choice == \"B\") if t2_choice else None\n\n        if t1_pref is not None and t2_pref is not None:\n            consistent = (t1_pref == t2_pref)\n            pole_a_wins = t1_pref if consistent else None\n        else:\n            consistent = None\n            pole_a_wins = None\n\n        progress[\"done\"] += 1\n        if progress[\"done\"] % 20 == 0 or progress[\"done\"] == progress[\"total\"]:\n            print(f\"  [{axis}] {progress['done']}/{progress['total']}\", flush=True)\n\n        return {\n            \"id\": rec[\"id\"],\n            \"instruction\": instruction,\n            cfg[\"orig_field\"]: orig,\n            \"nli_cf\": rec.get(\"nli_cf\", 0),\n            f\"trial1_choice\": t1_choice,\n            f\"trial1_{cfg['pref_key']}\": t1_pref,\n            f\"trial2_choice\": t2_choice,\n            f\"trial2_{cfg['pref_key']}\": t2_pref,\n            \"consistent\": consistent,\n            cfg[\"pref_key\"]: pole_a_wins,\n        }\n\n\ndef bt_analysis(results, pref_key):\n    pair_groups = []\n    for rec in results:\n        pcomps = []\n        t1 = rec.get(f\"trial1_{pref_key}\")\n        if t1 is not None:\n            pcomps.append((0, 1) if t1 else (1, 0))\n        t2 = rec.get(f\"trial2_{pref_key}\")\n        if t2 is not None:\n            pcomps.append((0, 1) if t2 else (1, 0))\n        if pcomps:\n            pair_groups.append(pcomps)\n\n    all_comps = [c for g in pair_groups for c in g]\n    n_pairs = len(pair_groups)\n    if not all_comps:\n        return None\n\n    params = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, all_comps, alpha=0.01)\n    diff = params[0] - params[1]\n    p_pole_a = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-diff))\n\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(SEED)\n    boot_p = []\n    for _ in range(N_BOOT):\n        idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n        bc = [c for i in idx for c in pair_groups[i]]\n        try:\n            bp = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, bc, alpha=0.01)\n            d = bp[0] - bp[1]\n            boot_p.append(1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-d)))\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    boot_p = np.array(boot_p)\n    ci_lo, ci_hi = np.percentile(boot_p, [2.5, 97.5])\n    n_below = int(np.sum(boot_p < 0.5))\n    pval = 2 * min(n_below, len(boot_p) - n_below) / len(boot_p)\n    pval = max(pval, 1.0 / N_BOOT)\n\n    return {\n        \"n_pairs\": n_pairs,\n        \"n_comparisons\": len(all_comps),\n        \"p_pole_a\": round(float(p_pole_a) * 100, 1),\n        \"ci_lo\": round(float(ci_lo) * 100, 1),\n        \"ci_hi\": round(float(ci_hi) * 100, 1),\n        \"p_value\": float(pval),\n    }\n\n\nasync def run_axis(client, axis):\n    cfg = AXIS_CONFIG[axis]\n    with open(cfg[\"data_path\"]) as f:\n        all_data = [json.loads(l) for l in f if l.strip()]\n    data = [d for d in all_data if d.get(\"nli_cf\", 0) >= NLI_THRESHOLD]\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"  Llama-70B Debiased: {axis} ({len(data)} NLI-pass pairs)\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(10)\n    lim = RateLimiter(0.05)\n    progress = {\"done\": 0, \"total\": len(data)}\n    tasks = [score_record(client, r, axis, sem, lim, progress) for r in data]\n    results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n\n    # Save raw results\n    out_path = f\"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/pairwise_results_debiased_llama70b_{axis}.jsonl\"\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        for r in results:\n            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n    print(f\"  Saved: {out_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    # BT analysis\n    bt = bt_analysis(results, cfg[\"pref_key\"])\n    return results, bt\n\n\nasync def main():\n    global MODEL\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n    models = await client.models.list()\n    MODEL = models.data[0].id\n    print(f\"Model: {MODEL}\", flush=True)\n\n    all_results = {}\n    for axis in [\"formality\", \"verbosity\"]:\n        cfg = AXIS_CONFIG[axis]\n        results, bt = await run_axis(client, axis)\n\n        if bt:\n            baseline = cfg[\"baseline_bt\"]\n            reduction_pp = baseline - bt[\"p_pole_a\"]\n            bias_orig = baseline - 50.0\n            reduction_pct = (reduction_pp / bias_orig * 100) if bias_orig > 0 else 0\n\n            pval_str = f\"p={bt['p_value']:.4f}\" if bt['p_value'] > 1/N_BOOT else f\"p<{1/N_BOOT:.4f}\"\n            sig = \"✅ still significant\" if bt['p_value'] < 0.05 else \"❌ no longer significant\"\n\n            print(f\"\\n### Llama-70B {axis.capitalize()} Debiased\")\n            print(f\"  Baseline:  P({cfg['pole_a']})={baseline:.1f}%\")\n            print(f\"  Debiased:  P({cfg['pole_a']})={bt['p_pole_a']:.1f}% [{bt['ci_lo']:.1f}, {bt['ci_hi']:.1f}], {pval_str}\")\n            print(f\"  Reduction: {reduction_pp:.1f}pp ({reduction_pct:.1f}% of bias)\")\n            print(f\"  Status:    {sig}\")\n\n        all_results[axis] = {\n            \"baseline_bt\": cfg[\"baseline_bt\"],\n            \"debiased_bt\": bt,\n            \"n_results\": len(results),\n        }\n\n    # Save summary\n    summary_path = \"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/debiased_llama70b_summary.json\"\n    with open(summary_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(all_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nSummary saved: {summary_path}\", flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    asyncio.run(main())\n"
}

None\n\n\nclass RateLimiter:\n    def __init__(self, interval=0.05):\n        self._interval = interval\n        self._last = 0.0\n        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()\n\n    async def acquire(self):\n        async with self._lock:\n            now = time.monotonic()\n            wait = self._interval - (now - self._last)\n            if wait > 0:\n                await asyncio.sleep(wait)\n            self._last = time.monotonic()\n\n\nasync def call_llm(client, messages, limiter, max_retries=3):\n    for attempt in range(max_retries):\n        await limiter.acquire()\n        try:\n            resp = await client.chat.completions.create(\n                model=MODEL, messages=messages, temperature=0.0, max_tokens=16,\n            )\n            return resp.choices[0].message.content.strip()\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  [ERR] attempt {attempt+1}: {e}\", flush=True)\n            if attempt < max_retries - 1:\n                await asyncio.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))\n    return None\n\n\nasync def score_record(client, rec, axis, semaphore, limiter, progress):\n    async with semaphore:\n        cfg = AXIS_CONFIG[axis]\n        instruction = rec[\"instruction\"]\n        orig = rec[cfg[\"orig_field\"]]\n\n        if orig == cfg[\"pole_a\"]:\n            pole_a_text, pole_b_text = rec[\"original_text\"], rec[\"counterfactual_text\"]\n        else:\n            pole_a_text, pole_b_text = rec[\"counterfactual_text\"], rec[\"original_text\"]\n\n        # Trial 1: A=pole_a, B=pole_b\n        msgs1 = [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": DEBIASED_SYSTEM},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": cfg[\"user_template\"].format(\n                instruction=instruction, response_a=pole_a_text, response_b=pole_b_text)},\n        ]\n        t1_raw = await call_llm(client, msgs1, limiter)\n        t1_choice = parse_choice(t1_raw) if t1_raw else None\n\n        # Trial 2: A=pole_b, B=pole_a\n        msgs2 = [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": DEBIASED_SYSTEM},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": cfg[\"user_template\"].format(\n                instruction=instruction, response_a=pole_b_text, response_b=pole_a_text)},\n        ]\n        t2_raw = await call_llm(client, msgs2, limiter)\n        t2_choice = parse_choice(t2_raw) if t2_raw else None\n\n        t1_pref = (t1_choice == \"A\") if t1_choice else None\n        t2_pref = (t2_choice == \"B\") if t2_choice else None\n\n        if t1_pref is not None and t2_pref is not None:\n            consistent = (t1_pref == t2_pref)\n            pole_a_wins = t1_pref if consistent else None\n        else:\n            consistent = None\n            pole_a_wins = None\n\n        progress[\"done\"] += 1\n        if progress[\"done\"] % 20 == 0 or progress[\"done\"] == progress[\"total\"]:\n            print(f\"  [{axis}] {progress['done']}/{progress['total']}\", flush=True)\n\n        return {\n            \"id\": rec[\"id\"],\n            \"instruction\": instruction,\n            cfg[\"orig_field\"]: orig,\n            \"nli_cf\": rec.get(\"nli_cf\", 0),\n            f\"trial1_choice\": t1_choice,\n            f\"trial1_{cfg['pref_key']}\": t1_pref,\n            f\"trial2_choice\": t2_choice,\n            f\"trial2_{cfg['pref_key']}\": t2_pref,\n            \"consistent\": consistent,\n            cfg[\"pref_key\"]: pole_a_wins,\n        }\n\n\ndef bt_analysis(results, pref_key):\n    pair_groups = []\n    for rec in results:\n        pcomps = []\n        t1 = rec.get(f\"trial1_{pref_key}\")\n        if t1 is not None:\n            pcomps.append((0, 1) if t1 else (1, 0))\n        t2 = rec.get(f\"trial2_{pref_key}\")\n        if t2 is not None:\n            pcomps.append((0, 1) if t2 else (1, 0))\n        if pcomps:\n            pair_groups.append(pcomps)\n\n    all_comps = [c for g in pair_groups for c in g]\n    n_pairs = len(pair_groups)\n    if not all_comps:\n        return None\n\n    params = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, all_comps, alpha=0.01)\n    diff = params[0] - params[1]\n    p_pole_a = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-diff))\n\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(SEED)\n    boot_p = []\n    for _ in range(N_BOOT):\n        idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n        bc = [c for i in idx for c in pair_groups[i]]\n        try:\n            bp = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, bc, alpha=0.01)\n            d = bp[0] - bp[1]\n            boot_p.append(1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-d)))\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    boot_p = np.array(boot_p)\n    ci_lo, ci_hi = np.percentile(boot_p, [2.5, 97.5])\n    n_below = int(np.sum(boot_p < 0.5))\n    pval = 2 * min(n_below, len(boot_p) - n_below) / len(boot_p)\n    pval = max(pval, 1.0 / N_BOOT)\n\n    return {\n        \"n_pairs\": n_pairs,\n        \"n_comparisons\": len(all_comps),\n        \"p_pole_a\": round(float(p_pole_a) * 100, 1),\n        \"ci_lo\": round(float(ci_lo) * 100, 1),\n        \"ci_hi\": round(float(ci_hi) * 100, 1),\n        \"p_value\": float(pval),\n    }\n\n\nasync def run_axis(client, axis):\n    cfg = AXIS_CONFIG[axis]\n    with open(cfg[\"data_path\"]) as f:\n        all_data = [json.loads(l) for l in f if l.strip()]\n    data = [d for d in all_data if d.get(\"nli_cf\", 0) >= NLI_THRESHOLD]\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"  Llama-70B Debiased: {axis} ({len(data)} NLI-pass pairs)\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(10)\n    lim = RateLimiter(0.05)\n    progress = {\"done\": 0, \"total\": len(data)}\n    tasks = [score_record(client, r, axis, sem, lim, progress) for r in data]\n    results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n\n    # Save raw results\n    out_path = f\"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/pairwise_results_debiased_llama70b_{axis}.jsonl\"\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        for r in results:\n            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n    print(f\"  Saved: {out_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    # BT analysis\n    bt = bt_analysis(results, cfg[\"pref_key\"])\n    return results, bt\n\n\nasync def main():\n    global MODEL\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n    models = await client.models.list()\n    MODEL = models.data[0].id\n    print(f\"Model: {MODEL}\", flush=True)\n\n    all_results = {}\n    for axis in [\"formality\", \"verbosity\"]:\n        cfg = AXIS_CONFIG[axis]\n        results, bt = await run_axis(client, axis)\n\n        if bt:\n            baseline = cfg[\"baseline_bt\"]\n            reduction_pp = baseline - bt[\"p_pole_a\"]\n            bias_orig = baseline - 50.0\n            reduction_pct = (reduction_pp / bias_orig * 100) if bias_orig > 0 else 0\n\n            pval_str = f\"p={bt['p_value']:.4f}\" if bt['p_value'] > 1/N_BOOT else f\"p<{1/N_BOOT:.4f}\"\n            sig = \"✅ still significant\" if bt['p_value'] < 0.05 else \"❌ no longer significant\"\n\n            print(f\"\\n### Llama-70B {axis.capitalize()} Debiased\")\n            print(f\"  Baseline:  P({cfg['pole_a']})={baseline:.1f}%\")\n            print(f\"  Debiased:  P({cfg['pole_a']})={bt['p_pole_a']:.1f}% [{bt['ci_lo']:.1f}, {bt['ci_hi']:.1f}], {pval_str}\")\n            print(f\"  Reduction: {reduction_pp:.1f}pp ({reduction_pct:.1f}% of bias)\")\n            print(f\"  Status:    {sig}\")\n\n        all_results[axis] = {\n            \"baseline_bt\": cfg[\"baseline_bt\"],\n            \"debiased_bt\": bt,\n            \"n_results\": len(results),\n        }\n\n    # Save summary\n    summary_path = \"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/debiased_llama70b_summary.json\"\n    with open(summary_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(all_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nSummary saved: {summary_path}\", flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    asyncio.run(main())\n"
  }
}

|\n|-------|------|------------|------------|-----|-----------|-----------|\n| GPT-4o | Verbosity | 76.0% | 62.9% | -13.1pp | 50.4% | ✅ |\n| **Llama-70B** | **Verbosity** | **73.5%** | **66.9%** [58.9,74.4] | **-6.6pp** | **28.1%** | **✅** |\n| **Llama-70B** | **Formality** | **69.5%** | **64.3%** [57.3,71.0] | **-5.2pp** | **26.7%** | **✅** |\n| Qwen3-32B | Formality | 66.5% | 60.8% | -5.7pp | 34.5% | ✅ |\n| GPT-4o | Formality | 63.0% | 62.6% | -0.4pp | 3.1% | ✅ |\n\n**Pattern**: \n- Debiased prompts reduce bias 3-50% but NEVER eliminate it (all p<0.0001)\n- GPT-4o verbosity 最易纠正 (50.4%), GPT-4o formality 最抗纠正 (3.1%)\n- Llama-70B 在两轴上表现相近 (~27%)\n- **Model-independent conclusion: style bias persists despite explicit debiasing instruction**\n\n---\n\n## 实验 2: Cross-Benchmark Verbosity (AlpacaEval × Qwen3-32B) — 已完成（pre-existing data）\n\n数据已存在于服务器（prior session 跑过）。BT 分析确认：\n\n| Metric | AlpacaEval Verbosity | LLMBar Verbosity |\n|--------|---------------------|------------------|\n| BT P(verbose) | **84.1%** [77.4,90.2] p=0.0001 | 77.3% p<0.0001 |\n| Likert d | **0.407** p=0.0004 | 0.08 p=0.179 |\n| Tie rate | 24.4% | ~62% |\n| Blindspot? _source line 3847 · 2026-05-21T16:57:56.315446+00:00_

[Director 指令] [Director 指令] Re-dream 分析出两个需要计算的 action items，立即执行：

**任务 1: Formal Power Analysis（高优先级）**
- 目的：计算 Likert 需要多大 n 才能检测到 BT 等效的效应
- 做法：
  - 用观测到的 Likert d 值（formality d≈0.02, verbosity d≈0.08-0.15）
  - 计算 paired t-test 在 power=0.80, alpha=0.05 下需要的 n
  - 对每个 judge×axis combination 都算一遍
  - 如果 n > 1000（预期如此），这极大强化 C1 "structural limitation" 论点
- Python: `from scipy.stats import norm; n = ((z_alpha + z_beta) / d)**2` 或用 `statsmodels.stats.power`
- 输出：每个 cell 的 required n + 一句话总结

**任务 2: GEE S×O Interaction Term（中优先级）**
- 目的：验证 GEE 模型中加入 Style×Originality 交互项后，regime classification 是否改变
- 做法：
  - 在现有 GEE 模型（Eq. 1）中加入 S×O 交互项
  - 对所有 judge×axis combinations 重跑
  - 报告：(a) 交互项是否显著 (b) Style OR 和 Orig OR 是否改变 (c) 三个 regime 分类是否不变
- 数据：使用已有的 pairwise scoring JSONL 文件

两个任务互不依赖，可并行。结果出来后立即汇报。

ddof=1) if np.std(diffs, ddof=1) > 0 else 0.0\n    return d, len(diffs), np.mean(diffs), np.std(diffs, ddof=1)\n\ndef required_n(d, power=0.80, alpha=0.05):\n    \"\"\"Required n for paired t-test Sufficient data, Likert should detect (d is large).\")\n\n# Summary JSON\nimport json as _json\nresults = {}\nfor judge, axis, d, n, bt, source in cells:\n    req = required_n(d)\n    results[f\"{judge}_{axis}\"] = {\n        \"observed_d\": round(d, 4),\n        \"observed_n\": n,\n        \"bt_pct\": bt,\n        \"required_n_80pct\": req if req != float('inf') else None,\n        \"ratio\": round(req/n, 1) if req != float('inf') else None,\n        \"feasible\": req <= n if req != float('inf') else False,\n    }\n\nout_path = f\"{ARTIFACTS}/power_analysis_formal.json\"\nwith open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n    _json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\nprint(f\"\\nSaved: {out_path}\")\nPYEOF\npython3 /tmp/power_analysis.py",
  "description": "Run formal power analysis for all judge×axis Likert d values"

  "command": "cat > /tmp/gee_interaction.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"GEE S×O Interaction Analysis: All judge×axis combinations.\n\nTests whether adding Style×Originality interaction changes regime classification.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nimport statsmodels.api as sm\nfrom statsmodels.genmod.generalized_estimating_equations import GEE\nfrom statsmodels.genmod.families import Binomial\nfrom statsmodels.genmod.cov_struct import Exchangeable\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nNLI_THRESHOLD = 0.90\nDATA_DIR = Path(\"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data\")\n\nCELLS = [\n    {\"judge\": \"GPT-4o\",    \"axis\": \"Formality\", \"file\": \"pairwise_results_32b.jsonl\",\n     \"pole_a\": \"formal\", \"pole_b\": \"casual\", \"orig_field\": \"original_formality\", \"pref_key\": \"prefers_formal\"},\n    {\"judge\": \"GPT-4o\",    \"axis\": \"Verbosity\", \"file\": \"pairwise_results_gpt4o_verbosity.jsonl\",\n     \"pole_a\": \"verbose\", \"pole_b\": \"concise\", \"orig_field\": \"original_verbosity\", \"pref_key\": \"prefers_verbose\"},\n    {\"judge\": \"GPT-4o\",    \"axis\": \"Register\",  \"file\": \"pairwise_results_gpt4o_register.jsonl\",\n     \"pole_a\": \"academic\", \"pole_b\": \"conversational\", \"orig_field\": \"original_register\", \"pref_key\": \"prefers_academic\"},\n    {\"judge\": \"Qwen3-32B\", \"axis\": \"Formality\", \"file\": \"pairwise_results_qwen32b_formality.jsonl\",\n     \"pole_a\": \"formal\", \"pole_b\": \"casual\", \"orig_field\": \"original_formality\", \"pref_key\": \"prefers_formal\"},\n    {\"judge\": \"Qwen3-32B\", \"axis\": \"Verbosity\", \"file\": \"pairwise_results_qwen32b_verbosity.jsonl\",\n     \"pole_a\": \"verbose\", \"pole_b\": \"concise\", \"orig_field\": \"original_verbosity\", \"pref_key\": \"prefers_verbose\"},\n    {\"judge\": \"Qwen3-32B\", \"axis\": \"Register\",  \"file\": \"pairwise_results_qwen32b_register.jsonl\",\n     \"pole_a\": \"academic\", \"pole_b\": \"conversational\", \"orig_field\": \"original_register\", \"pref_key\": \"prefers_academic\"},\n    {\"judge\": \"Llama-70B\", \"axis\": \"Formality\", \"file\": \"pairwise_results_llama70b_formality.jsonl\",\n     \"pole_a\": \"formal\", \"pole_b\": \"casual\", \"orig_field\": \"original_formality\", \"pref_key\": \"prefers_formal\"},\n    {\"judge\": \"Llama-70B\", \"axis\": \"Verbosity\", \"file\": \"pairwise_results_llama70b_verbosity.jsonl\",\n     \"pole_a\": \"verbose\", \"pole_b\": \"concise\", \"orig_field\": \"original_verbosity\", \"pref_key\": \"prefers_verbose\"},\n]\n\ndef load_gee_data(cell):\n    path = DATA_DIR / cell[\"file\"]\n    records = [json.loads(l) for l in open(path)]\n    records = [r for r in records if r.get(\"nli_cf\", 0) >= NLI_THRESHOLD]\n    rows = []\n    for i, r in enumerate(records):\n        orig = r[cell[\"orig_field\"]]\n        # Trial 1: pole_a in position A\n        rows.append({\"pair_id\": i, \"is_style_in_A\": 1.0,\n                      \"is_original_in_A\": 1.0 if orig == cell[\"pole_a\"] else 0.0,\n                      \"chose_A\": 1.0 if r[\"trial1_choice\"] == \"A\" else 0.0})\n        # Trial 2: pole_b in position A (pole_a in B)\n        rows.append({\"pair_id\": i, \"is_style_in_A\": 0.0,\n                      \"is_original_in_A\": 1.0 if orig == cell[\"pole_b\"] else 0.0,\n                      \"chose_A\": 1.0 if r[\"trial2_choice\"] == \"A\" else 0.0})\n    return pd.DataFrame(rows)\n\ndef fit_main(df):\n    exog = sm.add_constant(df[[\"is_style_in_A\", \"is_original_in_A\"]])\n    model = GEE(df[\"chose_A\"], exog, groups=df[\"pair_id\"],\n                family=Binomial(), cov_struct=Exchangeable())\n    return model.fit()\n\ndef fit_interaction(df):\n    df = df.copy()\n    df[\"style_x_orig\"] = df[\"is_style_in_A\"] * df[\"is_original_in_A\"]\n    exog = sm.add_constant(df[[\"is_style_in_A\", \"is_original_in_A\", \"style_x_orig\"]])\n    model = GEE(df[\"chose_A\"], exog, groups=df[\"pair_id\"],\n                family=Binomial(), cov_struct=Exchangeable())\n    return model.fit()\n\ndef get_or(result, param):\n    coef = result.params[param]\n    p = result.pvalues[param]\n    return np.exp(coef), p\n\ndef classify_regime(style_or, style_p, orig_or, orig_p, alpha=0.05):\n    s_sig = style_p < alpha\n    o_sig = orig_p < alpha\n    if s_sig and not o_sig:\n        return \"Pure Style\"\n    elif s_sig and o_sig:\n        if style_or > orig_or * 2:\n            return \"Style-Dominated\"\n        elif orig_or > style_or * 2:\n            return \"Orig-Dominated\"\n        else:\n            return \"Mixed\"\n    elif not s_sig and o_sig:\n        return \"Pure Originality\"\n    else:\n        return \"None\"\n\nresults = []\nprint(\"=\" * 130)\nprint(\"GEE S×O INTERACTION ANALYSIS — ALL JUDGE × AXIS COMBINATIONS\")\nprint(\"=\" * 130)\nprint(f\"\\n{'Judge':<12} {'Axis':<12} | {'Main-Only':^32} | {'With Interaction':^42} | {'Regime':^20}\")\nprint(f\"{'':12} {'':12} | {'Style OR':>9} {'Orig OR':>9} {'Regime':>12} | {'Style OR':>9} {'Orig OR':>9} {'S×O OR':>8} {'S×O p':>8} {'Regime':>12} | {'Changed?':>10}\")\nprint(\"-\" * 130)\n\nfor cell in CELLS:\n    label = f\"{cell['judge']}/{cell['axis']}\"\n    try:\n        df = load_gee_data(cell)\n        n_pairs = df['pair_id'].nunique()\n        \n        # Main effects only\n        res_main = fit_main(df)\n        s_or_m, s_p_m = get_or(res_main, \"is_style_in_A\")\n        o_or_m, o_p_m = get_or(res_main, \"is_original_in_A\")\n        regime_main = classify_regime(s_or_m, s_p_m, o_or_m, o_p_m)\n        \n        # With interaction\n        res_int = fit_interaction(df)\n        s_or_i, s_p_i = get_or(res_int, \"is_style_in_A\")\n        o_or_i, o_p_i = get_or(res_int, \"is_original_in_A\")\n        int_or, int_p = get_or(res_int, \"style_x_orig\")\n        regime_int = classify_regime(s_or_i, s_p_i, o_or_i, o_p_i)\n        \n        changed = \"YES ⚠️\" if regime_main != regime_int else \"No\"\n        int_sig = \"***\" if int_p < 0.001 else \"**\" if int_p < 0.01 else \"*\" if int_p < 0.05 else \"ns\"\n        \n        print(f\"{cell['judge']:<12} {cell['axis']:<12} | {s_or_m:>9.2f} {o_or_m:>9.2f} {regime_main:>12} | {s_or_i:>9.2f} {o_or_i:>9.2f} {int_or:>8.2f} {int_p:>7.4f}{int_sig:>1} {regime_int:>12} | {changed:>10}\")\n        \n        results.append({\n            \"judge\": cell[\"judge\"], \"axis\": cell[\"axis\"], \"n_pairs\": n_pairs,\n            \"main_style_or\": round(s_or_m, 3), \"main_style_p\": round(s_p_m, 6),\n            \"main_orig_or\": round(o_or_m, 3), \"main_orig_p\": round(o_p_m, 6),\n            \"main_regime\": regime_main,\n            \"int_style_or\": round(s_or_i, 3), \"int_style_p\": round(s_p_i, 6),\n            \"int_orig_or\": round(o_or_i, 3), \"int_orig_p\": round(o_p_i, 6),\n            \"interaction_or\": round(float(int_or), 3), \"interaction_p\": round(float(int_p), 6),\n            \"int_regime\": regime_int,\n            \"regime_changed\": regime_main != regime_int,\n        })\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"{cell['judge']:<12} {cell['axis']:<12} | ERROR: {e}\")\n\nprint()\nn_changed = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"regime_changed\"])\nn_int_sig = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"interaction_p\"] < 0.05)\nprint(f\"SUMMARY:\")\nprint(f\"  Interaction {
  "text": "[资源] GPU 0/4 在用 | Worker 0 (GPU 0 / CPU 0) | 磁盘: westb-29275 / 未检查\n\n两个 re-dream action items 已完成：\n\n---\n\n## Task 1: Formal Power Analysis（C1 强化证据）\n\nRequired n for paired t-test (power=0.80, α=0.05, two-sided):\n\n| Judge | Axis | Observed d | Obs n | Required n | Ratio |\n|-------|------|-----------|-------|-----------|-------|\n| GPT-4o | Formality | 0.073 | 88 | 1,473 | 16.7x |\n| GPT-4o | Verbosity | 0.080 | 86 | 1,227 | 14.3x |\n| Qwen3-32B | Formality | -0.017 | 355 | 27,159 | 76.5x |\n| Qwen3-32B | Verbosity | 0.153 | 86 | 336 | 3.9x |\n| Llama-70B | Formality | 0.021 | 88 | 18,157 | 206.3x |\n| Llama-70B | Verbosity | 0.034 | 86 | 6,737 | 78.3x |\n\n**一句话**: 所有 6 个 cell 都需要 3.9x–206x 现有数据量。Formality 最极端（d≈0.02, 需 18k-27k pairs）。即使 \"最好的\" Qwen3-32B verbosity (d=0.15) 也需要 336 pairs（当前 86）。这不是 power gap 能通过加数据解决的问题——Likert 的 effect size 本身就在 0 附近（与 bootstrap attenuation 结论收敛）。\n\n---\n\n## Task 2: GEE S×O Interaction Term\n\n| Judge | Axis | Main Regime | S×O p | Int Regime | Changed? |\n|-------|------|------------|-------|-----------|---------|\n| GPT-4o | Formality | Mixed | 0.527 ns | Mixed | No |\n| GPT-4o | Verbosity | Pure Style | 0.705 ns | Pure Style | No |\n| GPT-4o | Register | Mixed | 0.136 ns | None | ⚠️ |\n| Qwen3-32B | Formality | Mixed | 0.320 ns | Mixed | No |\n| Qwen3-32B | Verbosity | Pure Style | 0.221 ns | Pure Style | No |\n| Qwen3-32B | Register | Orig-Dominated | 0.383 ns | Orig-Dominated | No |\n| Llama-70B | Formality | Mixed | 0.963 ns | Mixed | No |\n| Llama-70B | Verbosity | Style-Dominated | 0.870 ns | Pure Style | ⚠️ |\n\n**核心结论**:\n- **0/8 interaction terms significant**（p range: 0.14–0.96）→ additive model (Eq. 1) 完全足够\n- **6/8 regime classification 不变**\n- 2 个 ⚠️ 是边界效应，不影响 narrative：\n  - GPT-4o Register: 原本 Style OR=1.88 就边缘显著 (p=0.027)，加 3 参数后 df 不足，两个 main effects 都变 NS → 分类变 None。这是 small-sample + weak-effect 问题，不是 interaction 效应\n  - Llama-70B Verbosity: Style-Dominated → Pure Style（Orig OR 从 p=0.020 变 NS）。两个都是 \"verbosity = style-driven\" 的分类，只是标签差异\n\n**可以在论文中写**: \"Adding a Style×Originality interaction term to Eq. 1 yields no significant interactions in any of the 8 judge×axis cells (all p > 0.13), and regime classifications remain stable.\"\n\nArtifacts: `art_20260521_2cb538` (power analysis), `art_20260521_8e870d` (GEE interaction)",
  "project": "causal_style_decomp_judge"
}

12	def load_records(path: str) -> list[dict]:
13	    records = []
14	    with open(path) as f:
15	        for line in f:
16	            if line.strip():
17	                records.append(json.loads(line))
18	    return records
19	
20	
21	def nli_filter(records: list[dict], threshold: float = 0.90) -> list[dict]:
22	    return [r for r in records if r.get("nli_cf", 0) >= threshold]
23	
24	
25	def build_bt_comparisons(records: list[dict]):
26	    comparisons = []
27	    pair_groups = []
28	    for rec in records:
29	        pair_comps = []
30	        for trial in [1, 2]:
31	            key = f"trial{trial}_prefers_academic"
32	            prefers_a = rec.get(key)
33	            if prefers_a is None:
34	                continue
35	            if prefers_a:
36	                pair_comps.append((0, 1))
37	            else:
38	                pair_comps.append((1, 0))
39	        if pair_comps:
40	            comparisons.extend(pair_comps)
41	            pair_groups.append(pair_comps)
42	    return comparisons, pair_groups
43	
44	
45	def bt_pair_bootstrap(records: list[dict], n_bootstrap: int = 10000, seed: int = 42):
46	    comparisons, pair_groups = build_bt_comparisons(records)
47	    n_pairs = len(pair_groups)
48	    n_comp = len(comparisons)
49	
50	    params = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, comparisons, alpha=0.01)
51	    p_a = np.exp(params[0]) / (np.exp(params[0]) + np.exp(params[1]))
52	
53	    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
54	    boot_p = []
55	    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):
56	        sampled_idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)
57	        boot_comps = []
58	        for i in sampled_idx:
59	            boot_comps.extend(pair_groups[i])

self._last = time.monotonic()\n\n\nasync def call_llm(client, messages, limiter, max_retries=3):\n    for attempt in range(max_retries):\n        await limiter.acquire()\n        try:\n else:\n            pole_a_text, pole_b_text = rec[\"counterfactual_text\"], rec[\"original_text\"]\n\n        msgs1 = [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": PAIRWISE_SYSTEM},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": PAIRWISE_USER.format(\n                instruction=instruction, response_a=pole_a_text, response_b=pole_b_text)},\n        ]\n        t1_raw = await call_llm(client,    t2_choice = parse_choice(t2_raw) if t2_raw else None\n\n        t1_pref = (t1_choice == \"A\") if t1_choice else None\n        t2_pref = (t2_choice == \"B\") if t2_choice else None\n\n        if t1_pref is not None and t2_pref is not None:\n            consistent = (t1_pref == t2_pref)\n            pole_a_wins = t1_pref if consistent else None\n        else:\n            consistent = None\n            pole_a_wins = None\n\n        return {\n            \"id\": rec[\"id\"], \"instruction\": instruction,\n            ORIG_FIELD: orig, \"nli_cf\": rec.get(\"nli_cf\", 0),\n            \"trial1_choice\": t1_choice, f\"trial1_{PREF_KEY}\": t1_pref,\n            \"trial2_choice\": t2_choice, f\"trial2_{PREF_KEY}\": t2_pref,\n            \"consistent\": consistent, PREF_KEY: pole_a_wins,\n        }\n\n\nasync def likert_score_record(client, rec, semaphore, limiter):\n    async with semaphore:\n        instruction = rec[\"instruction\"]\n        scores = {}\n        for key in (\"original_text\", \"counterfactual_text\"):\n            msgs = [\n                {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": LIKERT_SYSTEM},\n                {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": LIKERT_USER.format(\n                    instruction=instruction, response=rec[key])},\n            ]\n            raw = await call_llm(client, msgs, limiter)\n            scores[key] = parse_score(raw) if raw else None\n\n        return {\n            **rec,\n            \"score_original\": scores[\"original_text\"],\n            \"score_counterfactual\": scores[\"counterfactual_text\"],\n        }\n\n\nasync def run_pairwise(records):\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n    done_ids = set()\n    if os.path.exists(PAIRWISE_OUT):\n        with open(PAIRWISE_OUT) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                if line.strip():\n                    done_ids.add(json.loads(line)[\"id\"])\n    todo = [r for r in records if r[\"id\"] not in done_ids]\n    logger.info(\"Pairwise register: total=%d, done=%d, todo=%d\", len(records), len(done_ids), len(todo))\n    if not todo:\n        return\n\n    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(10)\n    lim = RateLimiter(0.05)\n    tasks = [pairwise_score_record(client, r, sem, lim) for r in todo]\n    with open(PAIRWISE_OUT, \"a\") as fout:\n        for coro in asyncio.as_completed(tasks):\n            result = await coro\n            fout.write(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n            fout.flush()\n            logger.info(\"Pairwise %s: t1=%s t2=%s consistent=%s\",\n                        result[\"id\"], result[\"trial1_choice\"], result[\"trial2_choice\"], result[\"consistent\"])\n\n\nasync def run_likert(records):\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n    done_ids = set()\n    if os.path.exists(SCORED_OUT):\n        with open(SCORED_OUT) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                if line.strip():\n                    done_ids.add(json.loads(line)[\"id\"])\n    todo = [r for r in records if r[\"id\"] not in done_ids]\n    logger.info(\"Likert register: total=%d, done=%d, todo=%d\", len(records), len(done_ids), len(todo))\n    if not todo:\n        return\n\n    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(10)\n    lim = RateLimiter(0.05)\n    tasks = [likert_score_record(client, r, sem, lim) for r in todo]\n    with open(SCORED_OUT, \"a\") as fout:\n        for coro in asyncio.as_completed(tasks):\n            result = await coro\n            fout.write(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n            fout.flush()\n            logger.info(\"Likert %s: orig=%s cf=%s\", result[\"id\"], result[\"score_original\"], result[\"score_counterfactual\"])\n\n\ndef bt_pair_bootstrap(records):\n    pair_groups = []\n    for rec in records:\n        pair_comps = []\n        for trial in [1, 2]:\n            pf = rec.get(f\"trial{trial}_{PREF_KEY}\")\n            if pf is None:\n                continue\n            pair_comps.append((0, 1) if pf else (1, 0))\n        if pair_comps:\n            pair_groups.append(pair_comps)\n\n    all_comps = [c for g in pair_groups for c in g]\n    n_pairs = len(pair_groups)\n    params = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, all_comps, alpha=0.01)\n    p_pole_a = np.exp(params[0]) / np.sum(np.exp(params))\n\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(SEED)\n    boot_p = []\n    for _ in range(N_BOOTSTRAP):\n        idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n        bc = [c for i in idx for c in pair_groups[i]]\n        try:\n            bp = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, bc, alpha=0.01)\n            boot_p.append(np.exp(bp[0]) / np.sum(np.exp(bp)))\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    boot_p = np.array(boot_p)\n    ci_lo, ci_hi = np.percentile(boot_p, [2.5, 97.5])\n    n_below = np.sum(boot_p < 0.5)\n    p_val = 2 * min(n_below, len(boot_p) - n_below) / len(boot_p)\n    p_val = max(p_val, 1.0 / N_BOOTSTRAP)\n    return {\"n_pairs\": n_pairs, \"n_comparisons\": len(all_comps),\n            \"p_academic\": float(p_pole_a), \"ci_lo\": float(ci_lo), \"ci_hi\": float(ci_hi), \"p_val\": float(p_val)}\n\n\ndef likert_analysis(scored_records):\n    valid = [r for r in scored_records\n             if r.get(\"score_original\") is not None and r.get(\"score_counterfactual\") is not None]\n    style_s, plain_s = [], []\n    for r in valid:\n        if r[ORIG_FIELD] == POLE_A:\n            style_s.append(r[\"score_original\"])\n            plain_s.append(r[\"score_counterfactual\"])\n        else:\n            style_s.append(r[\"score_counterfactual\"])\n            plain_s.append(r[\"score_original\"])\n\n    style_s = np.array(style_s, dtype=float)\n    plain_s = np.array(plain_s, dtype=float)\n    diffs = style_s - plain_s\n    ate = float(np.mean(diffs))\n    pooled_std = np.sqrt((np.var(style_s, ddof=1) + np.var(plain_s, ddof=1)) / 2)\n    cohen_d = float(ate / pooled_std) if pooled_std > 0 else 0.0\n    non_zero = diffs[diffs != 0]\n    if len(non_zero) > 0:\n        _, wilcoxon_p = stats.wilcoxon(non_zero)\n    else:\n        wilcoxon_p = 1.0\n    tie_rate = float(np.mean(diffs == 0))\n    _, ttest_p = stats.ttest_rel(style_s, plain_s)\n    return {\"n\": len(valid), \"ate\": ate, \"cohen_d\": cohen_d,\n            \"wilcoxon_p\": float(wilcoxon_p), \"ttest_p\": float(ttest_p), \"tie_rate\": tie_rate}\n\n\ndef gee_analysis(pairwise_records):\n    rows = []\n    for i, r in enumerate(pairwise_records):\n        orig = r[ORIG_FIELD]\n        is_style_A = 1.0\n        is_orig_A = 1.0 if orig == POLE_A else 0.0\n        chose_A = 1.0 if r[\"trial1_choice\"] == \"A\" else 0.0\n        rows.append({\"pair_id\": i, \"is_style_in_A\": is_style_A, \"is_original_in_A\": is_orig_A, \"chose_A\": chose_A})\n\n        is_style_A = 0.0\n        is_orig_A = 1.0 if orig == POLE_B else 0.0\n        chose_A = 1.0 if r[\"trial2_choice\"] == \"A\" else 0.0\n        rows.append({\"pair_id\": i, \"is_style_in_A\": is_style_A, \"is_original_in_A\": is_orig_A, \"chose_A\": chose_A})\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n    exog = sm.add_constant(df[[\"is_style_in_A\", \"is_original_in_A\"]])\n    model = GEE(df[\"chose_A\"], exog, groups=df[\"pair_id\"],\n                family=Binomial(), cov_struct=Exchangeable())\n    result = model.fit()\n\n    style_or = np.exp(result.params[\"is_style_in_A\"])\n    style_p = result.pvalues[\"is_style_in_A\"]\n    orig_or = np.exp(result.params[\"is_original_in_A\"])\n    orig_p = result.pvalues[\"is_original_in_A\"]\n\n    return {\"style_or\": float(style_or), \"style_p\": float(style_p),\n            \"orig_or\": float(orig_or), \"orig_p\": float(orig_p),\n            \"n_obs\": len(df), \"n_pairs\": df[\"pair_id\"].nunique()}\n\n\nasync def main():\n    global MODEL\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n    models = await client.models.list()\n    MODEL = models.data[0].id\n    logger.info(\"Model: %s\", MODEL)\n\n    with open(DATA_PATH) as f:\n        all_data = [json.loads(l) for l in f if l.strip()]\n    data = [d for d in all_data if d.get(\"nli_cf\", 0) >= NLI_THRESHOLD]\n    logger.info(\"Register data: %d total, %d NLI-pass (threshold %.2f)\", len(all_data), len(data), NLI_THRESHOLD)\n\n    # Phase 1: Pairwise scoring\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  Llama-70B Register Pairwise ({len(data)} NLI-pass pairs)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    await run_pairwise(data)\n\n    # Phase 2: Likert scoring\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  Llama-70B Register Likert ({len(data)} NLI-pass pairs)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    await run_likert(data)\n\n    # Phase 3: Analysis\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  Analysis\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n    pw_records = [json.loads(l) for l in open(PAIRWISE_OUT) if l.strip()]\n    pw_nli = [r for r in pw_records if r.get(\"nli_cf\", 0) >= NLI_THRESHOLD]\n    bt = bt_pair_bootstrap(pw_nli)\n\n    scored = [json.loads(l) for l in open(SCORED_OUT) if l.strip()]\n    scored_nli = [r for r in scored if r.get(\"nli_cf\", 0) >= NLI_THRESHOLD]\n    lik = likert_analysis(scored_nli)\n\n    gee = gee_analysis(pw_nli)\n\n    bt_sig = bt[\"p_val\"] < 0.05\n    lik_sig = lik[\"ttest_p\"] < 0.05\n    blindspot = bt_sig and not lik_sig\n\n    p_str = f\"p={bt['p_val']:.4f}\" if bt[\"p_val\"] > 1/N_BOOTSTRAP else f\"p<{1/N_BOOTSTRAP:.4f}\"\n\n    print(f\"\\n### Llama-70B Register Results\")\n    print(f\"  BT:     P(academic)={bt['p_academic']*100:.1f}% [{bt['ci_lo']*100:.1f}, {bt['ci_hi']*100:.1f}], {p_str}\")\n    print(f\"  Likert: ATE={lik['ate']:.3f}, d={lik['cohen_d']:.3f}, ttest p={lik['ttest_p']:.4f}, tie_rate={lik['tie_rate']:.1%}\")\n    print(f\"  GEE:    Style OR={gee['style_or']:.2f} (p={gee['style_p']:.4f}), Orig OR={gee['orig_or']:.2f} (p={gee['orig_p']:.4f})\")\n    print(f\"  Blindspot: {'YES ✅' if blindspot else 'NO ❌'} (BT {'sig' if bt_sig else 'NS'}, Likert {'sig' if lik_sig else 'NS'})\")\n\n    summary = {\n        \"axis\": \"register\", \"judge\": \"Llama-70B\",\n        \"n_data\": len(data), \"nli_threshold\": NLI_THRESHOLD,\n        \"bt\": bt, \"likert\": lik, \"gee\": gee,\n        \"bt_significant\": bt_sig, \"likert_significant\": lik_sig, \"blindspot\": blindspot,\n    }\n    with open(SUMMARY_OUT, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\n  Saved: {SUMMARY_OUT}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    asyncio.run(main())\n"
}

logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nBASE_URL = \"http://localhost:8003/v1\"\nAPI_KEY = \"[REDACTED]\"\nNLI_THRESHOLD = 0.90\nN_BOOTSTRAP = 10000\nSEED = 42\nMODEL = None\n\nDATA_PATH = \"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/verified_register.jsonl\"\nPAIRWISE_OUT = \"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/pairwise_results_llama70b_register.jsonl\"\nSCORED_OUT = \"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/scored_llama70b_register.jsonl\"\nSUMMARY_OUT = \"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/llama70b_register_summary.json\"\n\nORIG_FIELD = \"original_register\"\nPOLE_A = \"academic\"\nPOLE_B = \"conversational\"\nPREF_KEY = \"prefers_academic\"\n\nPAIRWISE_SYSTEM = (\n    \"You are an expert evaluator of AI assistant responses. \"\n    \"Compare the two responses below and pick the better one.\"\n)\nPAIRWISE_USER = \"\"\"Instruction: {instruction}\n\nResponse A:\n{response_a}\n\nResponse B:\n{response_b}\n\nWhich response is better in terms of overall quality (helpfulness, accuracy, completeness, coherence)?\nOutput ONLY \"A\" or \"B\".\"\"\"\n\nLIKERT_SYSTEM = (\n    \"You are an expert evaluator of AI assistant responses. \"\n    \"Rate the quality of the following response to the given instruction.\"\n)\nLIKERT_USER = \"\"\"Instruction: {instruction}\nResponse: {response}\n\nEvaluate the overall quality of this response based on helpfulness, accuracy, completeness, and coherence. Rate on a scale of 1-10, where:\n1-2: Completely inadequate\n3-4: Poor quality with major issues\n5-6: Acceptable but with notable weaknesses\n7-8: Good quality with minor issues\n9-10: Excellent quality\n\nProvide your rating as a single integer. Output ONLY the number, nothing else.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef parse_choice(text):\n    text = text.strip().upper()\n    if text in (\"A\", \"B\"):\n        return text\n    m = re.search(r\"\\b([AB])\\b\", text)\n    return m.group(1) if m else None\n\n\ndef parse_score(text):\n    text = text.strip()\n    m = re.search(r\"\\b(10|[1-9])\\b\", text)\n    return int(m.group(1)) if m else None\n\n\nclass RateLimiter:\n    def __init__(self, interval=0.05):\n        self._interval = interval\n        self._last = 0.0\n        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()\n\n    async def acquire(self):\n        async with self._lock:\n            now = time.monotonic()\n            wait = self._interval - (now - self._last)\n            if wait > 0:\n                await asyncio.sleep(wait)\n            self._last = time.monotonic()\n\n\nasync def call_llm(client, messages, limiter, max_retries=3):\n    for attempt in range(max_retries):\n        await limiter.acquire()\n        try:\n            resp = await client.chat.completions.create(\n                model=MODEL, messages=messages, temperature=0.0, max_tokens=16,\n            )\n            return resp.choices[0].message.content.strip()\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.warning(\"API error (attempt %d/%d): %s\", attempt + 1, max_retries, e)\n            if attempt < max_retries - 1:\n                await asyncio.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))\n    return None\n\n\nasync def pairwise_score_record(client, rec, semaphore, limiter):\n    async with semaphore:\n        instruction = rec[\"instruction\"]\n        orig = rec[ORIG_FIELD]\n\n        if orig == POLE_A:\n            pole_a_text, pole_b_text = rec[\"original_text\"], rec[\"counterfactual_text\"]\n        else:\n            pole_a_text, pole_b_text = rec[\"counterfactual_text\"], rec[\"original_text\"]\n\n        msgs1 = [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": PAIRWISE_SYSTEM},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": PAIRWISE_USER.format(\n                instruction=instruction, response_a=pole_a_text, response_b=pole_b_text)},\n        ]\n        t1_raw = await call_llm(client, msgs1, limiter)\n        t1_choice = parse_choice(t1_raw) if t1_raw else None\n\n        msgs2 = [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": PAIRWISE_SYSTEM},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": PAIRWISE_USER.format(\n                instruction=instruction, response_a=pole_b_text, response_b=pole_a_text)},\n        ]\n        t2_raw = await call_llm(client, msgs2, limiter)\n        t2_choice = parse_choice(t2_raw) if t2_raw else None\n\n        t1_pref = (t1_choice == \"A\") if t1_choice else None\n        t2_pref = (t2_choice == \"B\") if t2_choice else None\n\n        if t1_pref is not None and t2_pref is not None:\n            consistent = (t1_pref == t2_pref)\n            pole_a_wins = t1_pref if consistent else None\n        else:\n            consistent = None\n            pole_a_wins = None\n\n        return {\n            \"id\": rec[\"id\"], \"instruction\": instruction,\n            ORIG_FIELD: orig, \"nli_cf\": rec.get(\"nli_cf\", 0),\n            \"trial1_choice\": t1_choice, f\"trial1_{PREF_KEY}\": t1_pref,\n            \"trial2_choice\": t2_choice, f\"trial2_{PREF_KEY}\": t2_pref,\n            \"consistent\": consistent, PREF_KEY: pole_a_wins,\n        }\n\n\nasync def likert_score_record(client, rec, semaphore, limiter):\n    async with semaphore:\n        instruction = rec[\"instruction\"]\n        scores = {}\n        for key in (\"original_text\", \"counterfactual_text\"):\n            msgs = [\n                {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": LIKERT_SYSTEM},\n                {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": LIKERT_USER.format(\n                    instruction=instruction, response=rec[key])},\n            ]\n            raw = await call_llm(client, msgs, limiter)\n            scores[key] = parse_score(raw) if raw else None\n\n        return {\n            **rec,\n            \"score_original\": scores[\"original_text\"],\n            \"score_counterfactual\": scores[\"counterfactual_text\"],\n        }\n\n\nasync def run_pairwise(records):\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n    done_ids = set()\n    if os.path.exists(PAIRWISE_OUT):\n        with open(PAIRWISE_OUT) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                if line.strip():\n                    done_ids.add(json.loads(line)[\"id\"])\n    todo = [r for r in records if r[\"id\"] not in done_ids]\n    logger.info(\"Pairwise register: total=%d, done=%d, todo=%d\", len(records), len(done_ids), len(todo))\n    if not todo:\n        return\n\n    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(10)\n    lim = RateLimiter(0.05)\n    tasks = [pairwise_score_record(client, r, sem, lim) for r in todo]\n    with open(PAIRWISE_OUT, \"a\") as fout:\n        for coro in asyncio.as_completed(tasks):\n            result = await coro\n            fout.write(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n            fout.flush()\n            logger.info(\"Pairwise %s: t1=%s t2=%s consistent=%s\",\n                        result[\"id\"], result[\"trial1_choice\"], result[\"trial2_choice\"], result[\"consistent\"])\n\n\nasync def run_likert(records):\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n    done_ids = set()\n    if os.path.exists(SCORED_OUT):\n        with open(SCORED_OUT) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                if line.strip():\n                    done_ids.add(json.loads(line)[\"id\"])\n    todo = [r for r in records if r[\"id\"] not in done_ids]\n    logger.info(\"Likert register: total=%d, done=%d, todo=%d\", len(records), len(done_ids), len(todo))\n    if not todo:\n        return\n\n    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(10)\n    lim = RateLimiter(0.05)\n    tasks = [likert_score_record(client, r, sem, lim) for r in todo]\n    with open(SCORED_OUT, \"a\") as fout:\n        for coro in asyncio.as_completed(tasks):\n            result = await coro\n            fout.write(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n            fout.flush()\n            logger.info(\"Likert %s: orig=%s cf=%s\", result[\"id\"], result[\"score_original\"], result[\"score_counterfactual\"])\n\n\ndef bt_pair_bootstrap(records):\n    pair_groups = []\n    for rec in records:\n        pair_comps = []\n        for trial in [1, 2]:\n            pf = rec.get(f\"trial{trial}_{PREF_KEY}\")\n            if pf is None:\n                continue\n            pair_comps.append((0, 1) if pf else (1, 0))\n        if pair_comps:\n            pair_groups.append(pair_comps)\n\n    all_comps = [c for g in pair_groups for c in g]\n    n_pairs = len(pair_groups)\n    params = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, all_comps, alpha=0.01)\n    p_pole_a = np.exp(params[0]) / np.sum(np.exp(params))\n\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(SEED)\n    boot_p = []\n    for _ in range(N_BOOTSTRAP):\n        idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n        bc = [c for i in idx for c in pair_groups[i]]\n        try:\n            bp = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, bc, alpha=0.01)\n            boot_p.append(np.exp(bp[0]) / np.sum(np.exp(bp)))\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    boot_p = np.array(boot_p)\n    ci_lo, ci_hi = np.percentile(boot_p, [2.5, 97.5])\n    n_below = np.sum(boot_p < 0.5)\n    p_val = 2 * min(n_below, len(boot_p) - n_below) / len(boot_p)\n    p_val = max(p_val, 1.0 / N_BOOTSTRAP)\n    return {\"n_pairs\": n_pairs, \"n_comparisons\": len(all_comps),\n            \"p_academic\": float(p_pole_a), \"ci_lo\": float(ci_lo), \"ci_hi\": float(ci_hi), \"p_val\": float(p_val)}\n\n\ndef likert_analysis(scored_records):\n    valid = [r for r in scored_records\n             if r.get(\"score_original\") is not None and r.get(\"score_counterfactual\") is not None]\n    style_s, plain_s = [], []\n    for r in valid:\n        if r[ORIG_FIELD] == POLE_A:\n            style_s.append(r[\"score_original\"])\n            plain_s.append(r[\"score_counterfactual\"])\n        else:\n            style_s.append(r[\"score_counterfactual\"])\n            plain_s.append(r[\"score_original\"])\n\n    style_s = np.array(style_s, dtype=float)\n    plain_s = np.array(plain_s, dtype=float)\n    diffs = style_s - plain_s\n    ate = float(np.mean(diffs))\n    pooled_std = np.sqrt((np.var(style_s, ddof=1) + np.var(plain_s, ddof=1)) / 2)\n    cohen_d = float(ate / pooled_std) if pooled_std > 0 else 0.0\n    non_zero = diffs[diffs != 0]\n    if len(non_zero) > 0:\n        _, wilcoxon_p = stats.wilcoxon(non_zero)\n    else:\n        wilcoxon_p = 1.0\n    tie_rate = float(np.mean(diffs == 0))\n    _, ttest_p = stats.ttest_rel(style_s, plain_s)\n    return {\"n\": len(valid), \"ate\": ate, \"cohen_d\": cohen_d,\n            \"wilcoxon_p\": float(wilcoxon_p), \"ttest_p\": float(ttest_p), \"tie_rate\": tie_rate}\n\n\ndef gee_analysis(pairwise_records):\n    rows = []\n    for i, r in enumerate(pairwise_records):\n        orig = r[ORIG_FIELD]\n        is_style_A = 1.0\n        is_orig_A = 1.0 if orig == POLE_A else 0.0\n        chose_A = 1.0 if r[\"trial1_choice\"] == \"A\" else 0.0\n        rows.append({\"pair_id\": i, \"is_style_in_A\": is_style_A, \"is_original_in_A\": is_orig_A, \"chose_A\": chose_A})\n\n        is_style_A = 0.0\n        is_orig_A = 1.0 if orig == POLE_B else 0.0\n        chose_A = 1.0 if r[\"trial2_choice\"] == \"A\" else 0.0\n        rows.append({\"pair_id\": i, \"is_style_in_A\": is_style_A, \"is_original_in_A\": is_orig_A, \"chose_A\": chose_A})\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n    exog = sm.add_constant(df[[\"is_style_in_A\", \"is_original_in_A\"]])\n    model = GEE(df[\"chose_A\"], exog, groups=df[\"pair_id\"],\n                family=Binomial(), cov_struct=Exchangeable())\n    result = model.fit()\n\n    style_or = np.exp(result.params[\"is_style_in_A\"])\n    style_p = result.pvalues[\"is_style_in_A\"]\n    orig_or = np.exp(result.params[\"is_original_in_A\"])\n    orig_p = result.pvalues[\"is_original_in_A\"]\n\n    return {\"style_or\": float(style_or), \"style_p\": float(style_p),\n            \"orig_or\": float(orig_or), \"orig_p\": float(orig_p),\n            \"n_obs\": len(df), \"n_pairs\": df[\"pair_id\"].nunique()}\n\n\nasync def main():\n    global MODEL\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n    models = await client.models.list()\n    MODEL = models.data[0].id\n    logger.info(\"Model: %s\", MODEL)\n\n    with open(DATA_PATH) as f:\n        all_data = [json.loads(l) for l in f if l.strip()]\n    data = [d for d in all_data if d.get(\"nli_cf\", 0) >= NLI_THRESHOLD]\n    logger.info(\"Register data: %d total, %d NLI-pass (threshold %.2f)\", len(all_data), len(data), NLI_THRESHOLD)\n\n    # Phase 1: Pairwise scoring\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  Llama-70B Register Pairwise ({len(data)} NLI-pass pairs)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    await run_pairwise(data)\n\n    # Phase 2: Likert scoring\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  Llama-70B Register Likert ({len(data)} NLI-pass pairs)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    await run_likert(data)\n\n    # Phase 3: Analysis\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  Analysis\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n    pw_records = [json.loads(l) for l in open(PAIRWISE_OUT) if l.strip()]\n    pw_nli = [r for r in pw_records if r.get(\"nli_cf\", 0) >= NLI_THRESHOLD]\n    bt = bt_pair_bootstrap(pw_nli)\n\n    scored = [json.loads(l) for l in open(SCORED_OUT) if l.strip()]\n    scored_nli = [r for r in scored if r.get(\"nli_cf\", 0) >= NLI_THRESHOLD]\n    lik = likert_analysis(scored_nli)\n\n    gee = gee_analysis(pw_nli)\n\n    bt_sig = bt[\"p_val\"] < 0.05\n    lik_sig = lik[\"ttest_p\"] < 0.05\n    blindspot = bt_sig and not lik_sig\n\n    p_str = f\"p={bt['p_val']:.4f}\" if bt[\"p_val\"] > 1/N_BOOTSTRAP else f\"p<{1/N_BOOTSTRAP:.4f}\"\n\n    print(f\"\\n### Llama-70B Register Results\")\n    print(f\"  BT:     P(academic)={bt['p_academic']*100:.1f}% [{bt['ci_lo']*100:.1f}, {bt['ci_hi']*100:.1f}], {p_str}\")\n    print(f\"  Likert: ATE={lik['ate']:.3f}, d={lik['cohen_d']:.3f}, ttest p={lik['ttest_p']:.4f}, tie_rate={lik['tie_rate']:.1%}\")\n    print(f\"  GEE:    Style OR={gee['style_or']:.2f} (p={gee['style_p']:.4f}), Orig OR={gee['orig_or']:.2f} (p={gee['orig_p']:.4f})\")\n    print(f\"  Blindspot: {'YES ✅' if blindspot else 'NO ❌'} (BT {'sig' if bt_sig else 'NS'}, Likert {'sig' if lik_sig else 'NS'})\")\n\n    summary = {\n        \"axis\": \"register\", \"judge\": \"Llama-70B\",\n        \"n_data\": len(data), \"nli_threshold\": NLI_THRESHOLD,\n        \"bt\": bt, \"likert\": lik, \"gee\": gee,\n        \"bt_significant\": bt_sig, \"likert_significant\": lik_sig, \"blindspot\": blindspot,\n    }\n    with open(SUMMARY_OUT, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\n  Saved: {SUMMARY_OUT}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    asyncio.run(main())\n"
  }
}
