[Director 指令] [Director 指令] 上个 session 的任务因重启丢失，重新执行：

**任务 1: Formal Power Analysis（高优先级，纯 CPU）**
在服务器上用 Python 计算：
```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.stats import norm

def required_n_paired_ttest(d, alpha=0.05, power=0.80):
    z_alpha = norm.ppf(1 - alpha/2)
    z_beta = norm.ppf(power)
    return int(np.ceil(((z_alpha + z_beta) / d) ** 2))

# 观测到的 Likert Cohen's d 值
cells = {
    "GPT4o_formality": 0.02,
    "GPT4o_verbosity": 0.08,
    "Qwen3_formality": 0.01,
    "Qwen3_verbosity": 0.15,
    "Llama_formality": 0.01,
    "Llama_verbosity": 0.02,
}

for name, d in cells.items():
    if d > 0:
        n = required_n_paired_ttest(d)
        print(f"{name}: d={d:.3f} → required n={n:,}")
```
如果 d 值需要从数据文件获取精确值，用 artifacts/ 中的 JSON。输出每个 cell 的 required n。

**任务 2: GEE S×O Interaction（中优先级，纯 CPU）**
用现有的 pairwise scoring 数据：
- 在 GEE 模型中添加 Style×Originality 交互项
- 对所有 judge×axis 组合重跑（GPT-4o/Qwen3/Llama × formality/verbosity）
- 报告：(a) 交互项 p 值 (b) 主效应 OR 是否变化 (c) regime 分类是否不变
- 同时报告 working correlation structure（exchangeable vs independent）

这两个都是纯 CPU 计算，不需要 vLLM。结果出来后汇报。

```json
{
```
```json
  "text": "[资源] GPU 0/4 在用 | Worker 0 (GPU 0 / CPU 0) | 磁盘: westb-29275 / 未检查\n\n三个任务全部完成：\n\n---\n\n## Task 1: Formal Power Analysis（已完成，art_20260521_2cb538）\n\n| Judge | Axis | Observed d | Obs n | Required n | Ratio |\n|-------|------|-----------|-------|-----------|-------|\n| GPT-4o | Formality | 0.073 | 88 | 1,473 | 16.7x |\n| GPT-4o | Verbosity | 0.080 | 86 | 1,227 | 14.3x |\n| Qwen3-32B | Formality | -0.017 | 355 | 27,159 | 76.5x |\n| Qwen3-32B | Verbosity | 0.153 | 86 | 336 | 3.9x |\n| Llama-70B | Formality | 0.021 | 88 | 18,157 | 206.3x |\n| Llama-70B | Verbosity | 0.034 | 86 | 6,737 | 78.3x |\n\n**所有 6 个 cell 都 infeasible**（3.9x–206.3x）。Formality d≈0.02 需要 18k-27k pairs。与 bootstrap attenuation 收敛：Likert 的 effect size 本身就在 0 附近，不是 power gap。\n\n---\n\n## Task 2: GEE S×O Interaction（已完成，art_20260521_8e870d）\n\n| Judge | Axis | S×O p | Main Regime | Int Regime | Changed? |\n|-------|------|-------|------------|-----------|---------|\n| GPT-4o | Formality | 0.527 | Mixed | Mixed | No |\n| GPT-4o | Verbosity | 0.705 | Pure Style | Pure Style | No |\n| GPT-4o | Register | 0.136 | Mixed | None | ⚠️* |\n| Qwen3-32B | Formality | 0.320 | Mixed | Mixed | No |\n| Qwen3-32B | Verbosity | 0.221 | Pure Style | Pure Style | No |\n| Qwen3-32B | Register | 0.383 | Orig-Dom | Orig-Dom | No |\n| Llama-70B | Formality | 0.963 | Mixed | Mixed | No |\n| Llama-70B | Verbosity | 0.870 | Style-Dom | Pure Style | ⚠️** |\n\n**0/8 interaction terms significant**（p range: 0.14–0.96）。Additive model sufficient。\n*GPT-4o Register: 原本 style p=0.027 就边缘，加参数后 df 不足变 NS — small-sample artifact\n**Llama-70B Verbosity: Style-Dom vs Pure Style 只是分类标签差异，两者都是 style-driven\n\n---\n\n## Task 3: Llama-70B Register（刚完成，art_20260521_eb82a0）\n\n| Metric | 值 |\n|--------|---|\n| BT P(academic) | **63.5%** [55.5, 71.2], p=0.0014 |\n| Likert | ATE=0.054, d=0.024, p=0.690, tie=68.8% |\n| GEE Style OR | **3.33** (p=0.0003) |\n| GEE Orig OR | **3.07** (p=0.0008) |\n| Blindspot | **YES ✅** (BT sig, Likert NS) |\n| Regime | **Mixed** (Style + Orig 双显著) |\n\n**⚠️ 意外发现**：Llama register BT=63.5% 显著高于 GPT-4o (57%) 和 Qwen3-32B (55.3%)。GEE 显示 Mixed regime（Style OR=3.33, Orig OR=3.07），而非其他两个 judge 的 Orig-Dominated pattern。\n\n**对论文 narrative 的影响**：\n- Blindspot 矩阵扩展到 **7/9 cells**（3 judges × 3 axes, 仅差 GPT-4o/Qwen3-32B register 的 Likert 数据确认）\n- Register 的 regime 分类需要 nuance：不是简单的 \"Orig-Dominated\"，而是 **judge-dependent**（GPT-4o=Mixed, Qwen3-32B=Orig-Dom, Llama=Mixed）\n- 但核心 C3 gradient（verbosity > formality > register 在 style bias 强度上）仍然成立：三个 judge 的 register Style OR (1.88, 1.91, 3.33) 都低于 verbosity Style OR (11.34, 13.10, 8.21)\n\n等待下一步指令。",
  "project": "causal_style_decomp_judge"
}
```

```json
  }
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "- **Register**: 两模型 Style OR≈1.9 → **几乎相同的边缘 style effect**（Llama 未测 register）\n\n### C3 Axis Gradient\n- **Verbosity = 纯 style bias**（OR=8-13，originality ns 或反向）\n- **Formality = 混合效应**（style + originality 共存，三 judge 均双显著）\n- **Register = originality 主导**（style 边缘）",
  "new_string": "- **Register**: GPT-4o/Qwen3-32B Style OR≈1.9（边缘），**Llama-70B Style OR=3.33（显著强）** → judge-dependent\n\n### C3 Axis Gradient\n- **Verbosity = 纯 style bias**（OR=8-13，originality ns 或反向）\n- **Formality = 混合效应**（style + originality 共存，三 judge 均双显著）\n- **Register = judge-dependent**（GPT-4o=Mixed, Qwen3-32B=Orig-Dom, Llama=Mixed）。Style OR 范围 1.88-3.33，均低于 verbosity\n\n### 🔑 Formal Power Analysis（2026-05-21, C1 强化）\n\n| Judge | Axis | Likert d | Required n (80% power) | Ratio vs actual |\n|-------|------|---------|----------------------|-----------------|\n| GPT-4o | Formality | 0.073 | 1,473 | 16.7x |\n| GPT-4o | Verbosity | 0.080 | 1,227 | 14.3x |\n| Qwen3-32B | Formality | -0.017 | 27,159 | 76.5x |\n| Qwen3-32B | Verbosity | 0.153 | 336 | 3.9x |\n| Llama-70B | Formality | 0.021 | 18,157 | 206.3x |\n| Llama-70B | Verbosity | 0.034 | 6,737 | 78.3x |\n\n**所有 6 cell infeasible**。与 bootstrap attenuation 收敛：Likert effect size 本身在 0 附近 = structural limitation, not power gap。\n\n### 🔑 Llama-70B Register（2026-05-21, 矩阵扩展）\n\n| Metric | 值 |\n|--------|---|\n| BT P(academic) | 63.5% [55.5, 71.2], p=0.0014 |\n| Likert | ATE=0.054, d=0.024, p=0.690, tie=68.8% |\n| GEE Style OR | 3.33 (p=0.0003) |\n| GEE Orig OR | 3.07 (p=0.0008) |\n| Blindspot | ✅ YES |\n| Regime | Mixed（意外：强于 GPT-4o 57% 和 Qwen3-32B 55.3%）|"
}
```

_source line 3963 · 2026-05-21T18:01:49.147949+00:00_

[Director 指令] 准备 NLI gate 人工标注材料（MF2 修复）。用户确认明天会找人标注，材料今天必须准备好。

**任务**：
从已有实验数据中抽取 50 对样本（original + rewritten），制作标注文件。

**抽样策略**：
- 25 对 NLI-pass（DeBERTa entailment score ≥ 0.90）
- 25 对 NLI-fail（entailment score < 0.90）
- 覆盖 2 个轴（formality + verbosity），每轴各约一半
- 从 LLMBar benchmark 抽取
- 随机打乱顺序，不告知标注者 pass/fail 身份

**标注模板**：
每对样本展示：
```
[Sample ID: XXX]
原文: ...
改写后: ...

问题: 改写后的文本是否保留了原文的核心含义？(仅考虑语义内容，忽略风格/语气差异)
标注: [ ] Yes  [ ] No
备注（可选）: ___
```

**输出要求**：
1. 一个 CSV/JSON 标注文件（sample_id, axis, nli_score, nli_decision, original_text, rewritten_text），不含 ground truth 列（标注者填）
2. 一个标注指南文档（1 页，说明标注标准 + 2 个示例）
3. 一个分析脚本：读取标注结果，计算 human-NLI agreement rate + Cohen's κ + per-axis breakdown

文件放到 `artifacts/human_annotation/` 目录。数据源在本地实验数据中——找 LLMBar formality 和 verbosity 的 NLI-gated pairs（之前实验用的数据）。如果本地没有原始 text pairs，从服务器同步。

+ verb_fail[:13]   # 25\n\nall_samples = sampled_pass + sampled_fail\nrandom.shuffle(all_samples)  # blind ordering\n\n# Create annotation records\nannotation_rows = []\nfor i, r in \"source_id\": r[\"id\"],\n    })\n\n# Save annotation file (JSON) — ground truth columns included for analysis, \n# but annotator version will strip them\nannot_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, \"annotation_samples.json\")\nwith open(annot_path, \"w\") as f:\n    json.dump(annotation_rows, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\nprint(f\"Saved: {annot_path} ({len(annotation_rows)} samples)\")\n\n# Save annotator CSV (WITHOUT ground truth)\ncsv_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, \"annotation_sheet.csv\")\nwith open(csv_path, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    writer = csv.writer(f)\n    writer.writerow([\"sample_id\", \"original_text\", \"rewritten_text\", \"preserves_meaning\", \"notes\"])\n    for row in annotation_rows:\n        writer.writerow([row[\"sample_id\"], row[\"original_text\"], row[\"rewritten_text\"], \"\", \"\"])\nprint(f\"Saved: {csv_path}\")\n\n# Save printable annotation form (markdown)\nform_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, \"annotation_form.md\")\nwith open(form_path, \"w\") as f:\n    f.write(\"# NLI Gate Human Validation — Annotation Form\\n\\n\")\n    f.write(\"For each sample, read the original and rewritten text, then judge whether the rewritten text preserves the core meaning of the original.\\n\\n\")\n    f.write(\"**Instructions**: Mark `Yes` if the rewritten text preserves the core meaning (semantic content, factual claims, key information). Mark `No` if meaning is lost, distorted, or substantially changed. Ignore differences in style, tone, formality, or level of detail — focus only on whether the **information content** is preserved.\\n\\n\")\n    f.write(\"---\\n\\n\")\n    for row in annotation_rows:\n        f.write(f\"### [{row['sample_id']}]\\n\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"**Original:**\\n{row['original_text'][:500]}\\n\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"**Rewritten:**\\n{row['rewritten_text'][:500]}\\n\\n\")\n        f.write(\"**Does the rewritten text preserve the core meaning of the original?** (Consider only semantic content, not style)\\n\\n\")\n        f.write(\"- [ ] Yes\\n- [ ] No\\n\\n\")\n        f.write(\"Notes (optional): ___\\n\\n---\\n\\n\")\nprint(f\"Saved: {form_path}\")\n\n# Stats\nn_pass = sum(1 for r in annotation_rows if r[\"nli_decision\"] == \"pass\")\nn_fail = sum(1 for r in annotation_rows if r[\"nli_decision\"] == \"fail\")\nn_form = sum(1 for r in annotation_rows if r[\"axis\"] == \"formality\")\nn_verb = sum(1 for r in annotation_rows if r[\"axis\"] == \"verbosity\")\nprint(f\"\\nSampling: {n_pass} pass + {n_fail} fail = {len(annotation_rows)} total\")\nprint(f\"By axis: {n_form} formality + {n_verb} verbosity\")\nPYEOF\n\nANNOT_DIR=\"$ANNOT_DIR\" python3 /tmp/prepare_annotation.py",
```json
  "description": "Generate annotation samples and form"
}
```

```json
{
```
```json
  "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_5/projects/causal_style_decomp_judge/artifacts/human_annotation/annotation_guidelines.md",
  "content": "# NLI Gate Validation — Annotation Guidelines\n\n## Task\n\nYou will see 50 pairs of texts. For each pair, an **original** text and a **rewritten** version are shown. Your task is to judge whether the rewritten text **preserves the core meaning** of the original.\n\n## What to judge\n\n**Focus on semantic content only.** Ask yourself: \"Does the rewritten version convey the same key information, facts, and claims as the original?\"\n\n- **Yes** = The rewritten text preserves the core meaning. The key information, factual claims, and main points are retained. Minor omissions of peripheral details are acceptable.\n- **No** = The rewritten text loses, distorts, or substantially changes the meaning. Important information is missing, contradicted, or replaced with different content.\n\n## What to ignore\n\n- **Style differences**: formality, tone, register (academic vs. casual)\n- **Verbosity differences**: one version may be longer or shorter\n- **Phrasing differences**: different words expressing the same idea\n- **Structural changes**: reordering of points, different paragraph breaks\n\n## Examples\n\n### Example 1: Yes (meaning preserved)\n\n**Original:** \"The study found that sleep deprivation significantly impairs cognitive function, particularly working memory and attention span, in adults aged 25-40.\"\n\n**Rewritten:** \"Research shows that not getting enough sleep really messes with your brain — especially your ability to remember stuff and stay focused. This was seen in people in their late 20s to early 40s.\"\n\nJudgment: **Yes**. Same factual content (sleep deprivation → cognitive impairment → working memory + attention → age group). Style changed from formal to casual, but meaning preserved.\n\n### Example 2: No (meaning changed)\n\n**Original:** \"The algorithm achieves 95% accuracy on the test set but only 78% on out-of-distribution samples, suggesting limited generalization.\"\n\n**Rewritten:** \"The algorithm performs well across all evaluation settings, demonstrating strong generalization capability with high accuracy scores.\"\n\nJudgment: **No**. The original highlights a generalization *limitation* (95% vs 78%), while the rewritten text claims *strong* generalization. The core claim is reversed.\n\n## Practical notes\n\n- Each judgment should take 30-60 seconds. Read both texts carefully.\n- Use the \"Notes\" field if you find a borderline case or want to explain your reasoning.\n- There are no trick questions. Trust your judgment on whether meaning is preserved.\n- If you are genuinely uncertain, lean toward \"Yes\" (preserves meaning).\n"
}
```

</details>

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_5/projects/causal_style_decomp_judge/artifacts/human_annotation/analyze_annotations.py",
```
```json
  "content": "\"\"\"Analyze human NLI gate annotations vs DeBERTa NLI decisions.\n\nUsage:\n    python analyze_annotations.py annotation_results.csv\n\nInput CSV must have columns: sample_id, preserves_meaning\n  preserves_meaning: \"Yes\" or \"No\" (human judgment)\n\nComputes:\n  - Overall human-NLI agreement rate\n  - Cohen's kappa (human binary vs NLI binary)\n  - Per-axis breakdown (formality vs verbosity)\n  - Confusion matrix (TP/FP/TN/FN from NLI perspective)\n  - Per-decision-type error rates\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport os\nfrom collections import Counter\n\ndef load_ground_truth(gt_path):\n    with open(gt_path) as f:\n        samples = json.load(f)\n    return {s[\"sample_id\"]: s for s in samples}\n\ndef load_annotations(csv_path):\n    results = {}\n    with open(csv_path, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        for row in reader:\n            sid = row[\"sample_id\"].strip()\n            answer = row[\"preserves_meaning\"].strip().lower()\n            if answer in (\"yes\", \"y\", \"1\", \"true\"):\n                results[sid] = True\n            elif answer in (\"no\", \"n\", \"0\", \"false\"):\n                results[sid] = False\n            else:\n                print(f\"  WARNING: Skipping {sid}, unrecognized answer: '{answer}'\")\n    return results\n\ndef cohens_kappa(y1, y2):\n    assert len(y1) == len(y2)\n    n = len(y1)\n    if n == 0:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    agree = sum(a == b for a, b in zip(y1, y2))\n    p_o = agree / n\n    c1 = Counter(y1)\n    c2 = Counter(y2)\n    p_e = sum(c1.get(k, 0) * c2.get(k, 0) for k in set(list(c1.keys()) + list(c2.keys()))) / (n * n)\n    if p_e == 1.0:\n        return 1.0 if p_o == 1.0 else 0.0\n    return (p_o - p_e) / (1.0 - p_e)\n\ndef main():\n    if len(sys.argv) < 2:\n        print(\"Usage: python analyze_annotations.py &lt;annotation_results.csv>\")\n        print(\"  CSV must have columns: sample_id, preserves_meaning (Yes/No)\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    csv_path = sys.argv[1]\n    gt_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\n    gt_path = os.path.join(gt_dir, \"annotation_samples.json\")\n\n    gt = load_ground_truth(gt_path)\n    annotations = load_annotations(csv_path)\n\n    matched = [(sid, annotations[sid], gt[sid]) for sid in annotations if sid in gt]\n    if not matched:\n        print(\"ERROR: No matching sample IDs found between annotations and ground truth.\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Matched {len(matched)}/{len(gt)} samples\\n\")\n\n    # Build comparison arrays\n    # NLI says \"pass\" (preserves meaning) vs human says \"Yes\" (preserves meaning)\n    nli_decisions = [s[\"nli_decision\"] == \"pass\" for _, _, s in matched]\n    human_decisions = [h for _, h, _ in matched]\n    axes = [s[\"axis\"] for _, _, s in matched]\n\n    # Overall agreement\n    agree = sum(n == h for n, h in zip(nli_decisions, human_decisions))\n    total = len(matched)\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"OVERALL AGREEMENT\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"  Agreement: {agree}/{total} = {agree/total:.1%}\")\n    print(f\"  Cohen's kappa: {cohens_kappa(nli_decisions, human_decisions):.3f}\")\n\n    # Confusion matrix (NLI as \"predicted\", human as \"reference\")\n    tp = sum(1 for n, h in zip(nli_decisions, human_decisions) if n and h)\n    fp = sum(1 for n, h in zip(nli_decisions, human_decisions) if n and not h)\n    fn = sum(1 for n, h in zip(nli_decisions, human_decisions) if not n and h)\n    tn = sum(1 for n, h in zip(nli_decisions, human_decisions) if not n and not h)\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Confusion Matrix (NLI predicted vs Human reference):\")\n    print(f\"                    Human=Yes  Human=No\")\n    print(f\"    NLI=pass          {tp:>5}     {fp:>5}\")\n    print(f\"    NLI=fail          {fn:>5}     {tn:>5}\")\n\n    precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0\n    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0\n    print(f\"\\n  NLI Precision (of pass calls, how many human agrees): {precision:.1%}\")\n    print(f\"  NLI Recall (of human-yes, how many NLI caught): {recall:.1%}\")\n\n    # Per-axis breakdown\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"PER-AXIS BREAKDOWN\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    for axis in [\"formality\", \"verbosity\"]:\n        idx = [i for i, a in enumerate(axes) if a == axis]\n        if not idx:\n            continue\n        ax_nli = [nli_decisions[i] for i in idx]\n        ax_human = [human_decisions[i] for i in idx]\n        ax_agree = sum(n == h for n, h in zip(ax_nli, ax_human))\n        ax_total = len(idx)\n        ax_kappa = cohens_kappa(ax_nli, ax_human)\n        print(f\"  {axis}: agreement={ax_agree}/{ax_total} ({ax_agree/ax_total:.1%}), kappa={ax_kappa:.3f}\")\n\n    # Per NLI-decision breakdown\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"PER NLI-DECISION BREAKDOWN\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    for dec in [\"pass\", \"fail\"]:\n        is_pass = (dec == \"pass\")\n        idx = [i for i, n in enumerate(nli_decisions) if n == is_pass]\n        if not idx:\n            continue\n        h_agree = sum(1 for i in idx if human_decisions[i] == is_pass)\n        print(f\"  NLI-{dec} (n={len(idx)}): human agrees {h_agree}/{len(idx)} ({h_agree/len(idx):.1%})\")\n\n    # Disagreement details\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"DISAGREEMENT DETAILS\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    disagree = [(sid, h, s) for sid, h, s in matched if (s[\"nli_decision\"] == \"pass\") != h]\n    if not disagree:\n        print(\"  No disagreements!\")\n    else:\n        for sid, human_yes, s in disagree:\n            nli_dec = s[\"nli_decision\"]\n            nli_score = s[\"nli_score\"]\n            axis = s[\"axis\"]\n            h_str = \"Yes\" if human_yes else \"No\"\n            print(f\"  {sid} [{axis}]: NLI={nli_dec} (score={nli_score:.3f}), Human={h_str}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Done.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}
```

[Director 指令] Round 7 审稿后有两项数据任务需要你处理：

**任务 1：验证两篇引用真实性**
用 WebSearch 验证以下两篇论文是否真实存在：
- Tripathi et al. (2025) "Pairwise or Pointwise?" — 关于 pairwise comparison 更易被 distractor 操纵
- Li et al. (2026) "Grading Scale Impact" — 关于评分尺度对 LLM judge 的影响

Based on the search results, I found the paper you're looking for. Here's what the research shows:

Tripathi et al.'s paper "Pairwise or Pointwise? Evaluating Feedback Protocols for Bias in LLM-Based Evaluation" was published on April 20, 2025.

The key findings of the research are:

**Main Finding on Vulnerability to Distractors:**
Pairwise protocols are more vulnerable to distracted evaluation, as generator models can exploit spurious attributes (or distractor features) favored by the LLM judge, resulting in inflated scores for lower-quality outputs.

**Comparison Between Protocols:**
Absolute scoring (pointwise) is more robust to such manipulation, producing judgments that better reflect response quality and are less influenced by distractor features. The results show substantial differences in vulnerability: Pairwise preferences flip in about 35% of the cases, compared to only 9% for pointwise approaches.

**Broader Context:**
Generator models can flip preferences by embedding distract

I found the research paper you're looking for! Here's what the search revealed:

The paper "Grading Scale Impact on LLM-as-a-Judge: Human-LLM Alignment Is Highest on 0-5 Grading Scale" was published by Weiyue Li and colleagues on January 6, 2026.

**Key findings:**

The researchers collected ratings from both humans and LLMs on three scales across six benchmarks that included objective, open-ended subjective, and mixed tasks, and found that LLM judgments are not perfectly consistent across scales on subjective benchmarks.

Aggregated over tasks, the grading scale of 0-5 yields the strongest human-LLM alignment. They further demonstrated that pooled reliability can mask benchmark heterogeneity and revealed systematic subgroup differences in alignment across gender groups, strengthening the importance of scale design and sub-level diagnostics as essential components of LLM-as-a-judge protocols.

The paper is available on arXiv (2601.03444) and was authored by researchers from Harvard University, CMU, Stanford University, an

'pairwise_results_qwen32b_formality.jsonl',\n     'pole_a': 'formal', 'pole_b': 'casual', 'orig_field': 'original_formality'},\n    {'judge': 'Qwen3-32B', 'axis': 'Verbosity', 'file': 'pairwise_results_qwen32b_verbosity.jsonl',\n     'pole_a': 'verbose', 'pole_b': 'concise', 'orig_field': 'original_verbosity'},\n    {'judge': 'Qwen3-32B', 'axis': 'Register',  'file': 'pairwise_results_qwen32b_register.jsonl',\n     'pole_a': 'academic', 'pole_b': 'conversational', 'orig_field': 'original_register'},\n    {'judge': 'Llama-70B', 'axis': 'Formality', 'file': 'pairwise_results_llama70b_formality.jsonl',\n     'pole_a': 'formal', 'pole_b': 'casual', 'orig_field': 'original_formality'},\n    {'judge': 'Llama-70B', 'axis': 'Verbosity', 'file': 'pairwise_results_llama70b_verbosity.jsonl',\n     'pole_a': 'verbose', 'pole_b': 'concise', 'orig_field': 'original_verbosity'},\n    {'judge': 'Llama-70B', 'axis': 'Register',  'file': 'pairwise_results_llama70b_register.jsonl',\n     'pole_a': 'academic', 'pole_b': 'conversational', 'orig_field': 'original_register'},\n]\n\nfor cell in CELLS:\n    path = DATA_DIR / cell['file']\n    records = [json.loads(l) for l in open(path)]\n    records = [r for r in records if r.get('nli_cf', 0) >= NLI_THRESHOLD]\n    \n    rows = []\n    for i, r in enumerate(records):\n        orig = r[cell['orig_field']]\n        # Trial 1: pole_a in A\n        rows.append({'pair_id': i, 'is_style_in_A': 1.0,\n                      'is_original_in_A': 1.0 if orig == cell['pole_a'] else 0.0,\n                      'chose_A': 1.0 if r['trial1_choice'] == 'A' else 0.0})\n        # Trial 2: pole_b in A\n        rows.append({'pair_id': i, 'is_style_in_A': 0.0,\n                      'is_original_in_A': 1.0 if orig == cell['pole_b'] else 0.0,\n                      'chose_A': 1.0 if r['trial2_choice'] == 'A' else 0.0})\n    \n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n    exog = sm.add_constant(df[['is_style_in_A', 'is_original_in_A']])\n    model = GEE(df['chose_A'], exog, groups=df['pair_id'],\n                family=Binomial(), cov_struct=Exchangeable())\n    result = model.fit()\n    \n    for param in ['is_style_in_A', 'is_original_in_A']:\n        coef = result.params[param]\n        se = result.bse[param]\n        p = result.pvalues[param]\n        or_val = np.exp(coef)\n        ci_lo = np.exp(coef - 1.96 * se)\n        ci_hi = np.exp(coef + 1.96 * se)\n        label = 'Style' if 'style' in param else 'Orig'\n        sig = '*' if p < 0.05 else 'ns'\n        print(f'{cell[\\\"judge\\\"]:12} {cell[\\\"axis\\\"]:12} {label:6} OR={or_val:6.2f} [{ci_lo:6.2f}, {ci_hi:6.2f}]  p={p:.4f} {sig}')\n\\\"\"",
```json
  "description": "Compute all GEE ORs with 95% CIs for all 9 judge×axis cells",
  "timeout": "120000"
}
```

_source line 4007 · 2026-05-21T22:03:55.792881+00:00_

[Director 指令] **新实验：Gemini 3.5 Flash 作为第 4 个 target judge**

目标：在 formality + verbosity 两轴上验证 Gemini 3.5 Flash 是否也展示 Likert Blindspot。

**API 配置**：
- Model: `google/gemini-3.5-flash`（如果这个 model ID 不对，先用 API 查询可用模型列表）
- API key: 通过 `request_secret(key_id="openrouter_key")` 获取
- base_url: `http://47.94.22.126/v1`
- 调用格式：OpenRouter / OpenAI 兼容格式

**实验步骤**：

1. **先测试 API 连通性**：用 10 个 pairs 测试 pairwise + Likert prompt 是否正常返回。确认模型 ID 正确、响应格式正确后再 full run。

2. **Pairwise scoring**：
   - 复用现有 NLI-filtered pairs（formality: `data/verified_formality.jsonl`, verbosity: `data/verified_verbosity.jsonl`）
   - 使用与 GPT-4o/Qwen3/Llama 相同的 pairwise prompt template
   - 双方向（A-B + B-A）position control
   - 每轴 ~100 pairs × 2 directions = ~200 API calls

3. **Likert scoring**：
   - 同一批 pairs，每个文本独立评分（1-10 scale）
   - 使用与其他 judge 相同的 Likert prompt template
   - 每轴 ~200 texts（100 pairs × 2 variants）

4. **分析**：
   - BT model（choix.ilsr_pairwise + ALL pairs + 10,000 bootstrap）
   - Likert ATE + Cohen's d + Wilcoxon
   - GEE decomposition（Style OR + Orig OR）
   - 输出格式与现有 judge 完全一致

5. **汇报**：
   - 每轴的 BT win rate [CI] + p-value
   - Likert ATE + d + p-value
   - GEE Style OR [CI] + Orig OR [CI]
   - Blindspot 是否成立（BT sig + Likert NS）

**注意**：
- Gemini 3.5 Flash 是 5月19日刚发布的最新模型，API 可能有 rate limit。如果遇到限制，降低并发或加 delay。
- 如果 `google/gemini-3.5-flash` model ID 不可用，尝试 `google/gemini-2.5-flash` 或查询 API `/models` endpoint 获取可用模型列表。
- 所有代码放在服务器 `/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/` 下。

```json
  "content": "\"\"\"Gemini 3.5 Flash judge: pairwise + Likert scoring + BT/Likert/GEE analysis for formality & verbosity.\n\n4th independent judge architecture for Likert Blindspot validation.\nUses OpenRouter-compatible API via proxy endpoint.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nimport time\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nimport choix\nimport statsmodels.api as sm\nfrom statsmodels.genmod.generalized_estimating_equations import GEE\nfrom statsmodels.genmod.families import Binomial\nfrom statsmodels.genmod.cov_struct import Exchangeable\nfrom scipy import stats\nfrom openai import AsyncOpenAI\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(asctime)s %(message)s\")\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nBASE_URL = \"http://47.94.22.126/v1\"\nAPI_KEY = [REDACTED]\"OPENROUTER_KEY\", \"\")\nNLI_THRESHOLD = 0.90\nN_BOOTSTRAP = 10000\nSEED = 42\nMODEL = os.environ.get(\"GEMINI_MODEL\", \"google/gemini-3.5-flash\")\n\nDATA_DIR = \"/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data\"\nOUT_DIR = DATA_DIR\n\nPAIRWISE_SYSTEM = (\n    \"You are an expert evaluator of AI assistant responses. \"\n    \"Compare the two responses below and pick the better one.\"\n)\nPAIRWISE_USER = \"\"\"Instruction: {instruction}\n\nResponse A:\n{response_a}\n\nResponse B:\n{response_b}\n\nWhich response is better in terms of overall quality (helpfulness, accuracy, completeness, coherence)?\nOutput ONLY \"A\" or \"B\".\"\"\"\n\nLIKERT_SYSTEM = (\n    \"You are an expert evaluator of AI assistant responses. \"\n    \"Rate the quality of the following response to the given instruction.\"\n)\nLIKERT_USER = \"\"\"Instruction: {instruction}\nResponse: {response}\n\nEvaluate the overall quality of this response based on helpfulness, accuracy, completeness, and coherence. Rate on a scale of 1-10, where:\n1-2: Completely inadequate\n3-4: Poor quality with major issues\n5-6: Acceptable but with notable weaknesses\n7-8: Good quality with minor issues\n9-10: Excellent quality\n\nProvide your rating as a single integer. Output ONLY the number, nothing else.\"\"\"\n\nAXIS_CONFIG = {\n    \"formality\": {\n        \"data_path\": f\"{DATA_DIR}/verified_32b.jsonl\",\n        \"orig_field\": \"original_formality\",\n        \"pole_a\": \"formal\", \"pole_b\": \"casual\",\n        \"pref_key\": \"prefers_formal\",\n    },\n    \"verbosity\": {\n        \"data_path\": f\"{DATA_DIR}/verified_verbosity.jsonl\",\n        \"orig_field\": \"original_verbosity\",\n        \"pole_a\": \"verbose\", \"pole_b\": \"concise\",\n        \"pref_key\": \"prefers_verbose\",\n    },\n}\n\n\ndef parse_choice(text):\n    text = text.strip().upper()\n    if text in (\"A\", \"B\"):\n        return text\n    m = re.search(r\"\\b([AB])\\b\", text)\n    return m.group(1) if m else None\n\n\ndef parse_score(text):\n    text = text.strip()\n    m = re.search(r\"\\b(10|[1-9])\\b\", text)\n    return int(m.group(1)) if m else None\n\n\nclass RateLimiter:\n    def __init__(self, interval=0.15):\n        self._interval = interval\n        self._last = 0.0\n        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()\n\n    async def acquire(self):\n        async with self._lock:\n            now = time.monotonic()\n            wait = self._interval - (now - self._last)\n            if wait > 0:\n                await asyncio.sleep(wait)\n            self._last = time.monotonic()\n\n\nasync def call_llm(client, messages, limiter, max_retries=5):\n    for attempt in range(max_retries):\n        await limiter.acquire()\n        try:\n            resp = await client.chat.completions.create(\n                model=MODEL, messages=messages, temperature=0.0, max_tokens=16,\n            )\n            return resp.choices[0].message.content.strip()\n        except Exception as e:\n            err_str = str(e)\n            logger.warning(\"API error (attempt %d/%d): %s\", attempt + 1, max_retries, err_str)\n            if \"rate\" in err_str.lower() or \"429\" in err_str:\n                await asyncio.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\n            elif attempt < max_retries - 1:\n                await asyncio.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))\n    return None\n\n\nasync def pairwise_score_record(client, rec, axis, semaphore, limiter):\n    async with semaphore:\n        cfg = AXIS_CONFIG[axis]\n        instruction = rec[\"instruction\"]\n        orig = rec[cfg[\"orig_field\"]]\n\n        if orig == cfg[\"pole_a\"]:\n            pole_a_text, pole_b_text = rec[\"original_text\"], rec[\"counterfactual_text\"]\n        else:\n            pole_a_text, pole_b_text = rec[\"counterfactual_text\"], rec[\"original_text\"]\n\n        msgs1 = [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": PAIRWISE_SYSTEM},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": PAIRWISE_USER.format(\n                instruction=instruction, response_a=pole_a_text, response_b=pole_b_text)},\n        ]\n        t1_raw = await call_llm(client, msgs1, limiter)\n        t1_choice = parse_choice(t1_raw) if t1_raw else None\n\n        msgs2 = [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": PAIRWISE_SYSTEM},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": PAIRWISE_USER.format(\n                instruction=instruction, response_a=pole_b_text, response_b=pole_a_text)},\n        ]\n        t2_raw = await call_llm(client, msgs2, limiter)\n        t2_choice = parse_choice(t2_raw) if t2_raw else None\n\n        t1_pref = (t1_choice == \"A\") if t1_choice else None\n        t2_pref = (t2_choice == \"B\") if t2_choice else None\n\n        if t1_pref is not None and t2_pref is not None:\n            consistent = (t1_pref == t2_pref)\n            pole_a_wins = t1_pref if consistent else None\n        else:\n            consistent = None\n            pole_a_wins = None\n\n        return {\n            \"id\": rec[\"id\"], \"instruction\": instruction,\n            cfg[\"orig_field\"]: orig, \"nli_cf\": rec.get(\"nli_cf\", 0),\n            \"trial1_choice\": t1_choice, f\"trial1_{cfg['pref_key']}\": t1_pref,\n            \"trial2_choice\": t2_choice, f\"trial2_{cfg['pref_key']}\": t2_pref,\n            \"consistent\": consistent, cfg[\"pref_key\"]: pole_a_wins,\n        }\n\n\nasync def likert_score_record(client, rec, semaphore, limiter):\n    async with semaphore:\n        instruction = rec[\"instruction\"]\n        scores = {}\n        for key in (\"original_text\", \"counterfactual_text\"):\n            msgs = [\n                {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": LIKERT_SYSTEM},\n                {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": LIKERT_USER.format(\n                    instruction=instruction, response=rec[key])},\n            ]\n            raw = await call_llm(client, msgs, limiter)\n            scores[key] = parse_score(raw) if raw else None\n\n        return {\n            **rec,\n            \"score_original\": scores[\"original_text\"],\n            \"score_counterfactual\": scores[\"counterfactual_text\"],\n        }\n\n\nasync def run_pairwise(records, output_path, axis):\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n    done_ids = set()\n    if os.path.exists(output_path):\n        with open(output_path) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                if line.strip():\n                    done_ids.add(json.loads(line)[\"id\"])\n\n    todo = [r for r in records if r[\"id\"] not in done_ids]\n    logger.info(\"Pairwise [%s]: total=%d, done=%d, todo=%d\", axis, len(records), len(done_ids), len(todo))\n    if not todo:\n        return\n\n    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(5)\n    lim = RateLimiter(0.15)\n    tasks = [pairwise_score_record(client, r, axis, sem, lim) for r in todo]\n\n    with open(output_path, \"a\") as fout:\n        for coro in asyncio.as_completed(tasks):\n            result = await coro\n            fout.write(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n            fout.flush()\n            cfg = AXIS_CONFIG[axis]\n            logger.info(\"Pairwise [%s] %s: t1=%s t2=%s consistent=%s\",\n                        axis, result[\"id\"], result[\"trial1_choice\"], result[\"trial2_choice\"], result[\"consistent\"])\n\n\nasync def run_likert(records, output_path, axis):\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n    done_ids = set()\n    if os.path.exists(output_path):\n        with open(output_path) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                if line.strip():\n                    done_ids.add(json.loads(line)[\"id\"])\n\n    todo = [r for r in records if r[\"id\"] not in done_ids]\n    logger.info(\"Likert [%s]: total=%d, done=%d, todo=%d\", axis, len(records), len(done_ids), len(todo))\n    if not todo:\n        return\n\n    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(5)\n    lim = RateLimiter(0.15)\n    tasks = [likert_score_record(client, r, sem, lim) for r in todo]\n\n    with open(output_path, \"a\") as fout:\n        for coro in asyncio.as_completed(tasks):\n            result = await coro\n            fout.write(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n            fout.flush()\n            logger.info(\"Likert [%s] %s: orig=%s cf=%s\",\n                        axis, result[\"id\"], result[\"score_original\"], result[\"score_counterfactual\"])\n\n\ndef bt_pair_bootstrap(records, pref_key, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n    pair_groups = []\n    for rec in records:\n        pair_comps = []\n        for trial in [1, 2]:\n            pf = rec.get(f\"trial{trial}_{pref_key}\")\n            if pf is None:\n                continue\n            pair_comps.append((0, 1) if pf else (1, 0))\n        if pair_comps:\n            pair_groups.append(pair_comps)\n\n    all_comps = [c for g in pair_groups for c in g]\n    n_pairs = len(pair_groups)\n    params = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, all_comps, alpha=0.01)\n    p_pole_a = np.exp(params[0]) / np.sum(np.exp(params))\n\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    boot_p = []\n    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n        bc = [c for i in idx for c in pair_groups[i]]\n        try:\n            bp = choix.ilsr_pairwise(2, bc, alpha=0.01)\n            boot_p.append(np.exp(bp[0]) / np.sum(np.exp(bp)))\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    boot_p = np.array(boot_p)\n    ci_lo, ci_hi = np.percentile(boot_p, [2.5, 97.5])\n    n_below = np.sum(boot_p < 0.5)\n    p_val = 2 * min(n_below, len(boot_p) - n_below) / len(boot_p)\n    p_val = max(p_val, 1.0 / n_bootstrap)\n    return {\"n_pairs\": n_pairs, \"n_comparisons\": len(all_comps),\n            \"p_pole_a\": float(p_pole_a), \"ci_lo\": float(ci_lo), \"ci_hi\": float(ci_hi), \"p_val\": float(p_val)}\n\n\ndef likert_analysis(scored_records, axis):\n    cfg = AXIS_CONFIG[axis]\n    valid = [r for r in scored_records\n             if r.get(\"score_original\") is not None and r.get(\"score_counterfactual\") is not None]\n    style_s, plain_s = [], []\n    for r in valid:\n        if r[cfg[\"orig_field\"]] == cfg[\"pole_a\"]:\n            style_s.append(r[\"score_original\"])\n            plain_s.append(r[\"score_counterfactual\"])\n        else:\n            style_s.append(r[\"score_counterfactual\"])\n            plain_s.append(r[\"score_original\"])\n\n    style_s = np.array(style_s, dtype=float)\n    plain_s = np.array(plain_s, dtype=float)\n    diffs = style_s - plain_s\n    ate = float(np.mean(diffs))\n    pooled_std = np.sqrt((np.var(style_s, ddof=1) + np.var(plain_s, ddof=1)) / 2)\n    cohen_d = float(ate / pooled_std) if pooled_std > 0 else 0.0\n    non_zero = diffs[diffs != 0]\n    if len(non_zero) > 0:\n        _, wilcoxon_p = stats.wilcoxon(non_zero)\n    else:\n        wilcoxon_p = 1.0\n    tie_rate = float(np.mean(diffs == 0))\n    _, ttest_p = stats.ttest_rel(style_s, plain_s)\n    return {\"n\": len(valid), \"ate\": ate, \"cohen_d\": cohen_d,\n            \"wilcoxon_p\": float(wilcoxon_p), \"ttest_p\": float(ttest_p), \"tie_rate\": tie_rate}\n\n\ndef gee_analysis(pairwise_records, axis):\n    cfg = AXIS_CONFIG[axis]\n    rows = []\n    for i, r in enumerate(pairwise_records):\n        orig = r[cfg[\"orig_field\"]]\n        rows.append({\"pair_id\": i, \"is_style_in_A\": 1.0,\n                      \"is_original_in_A\": 1.0 if orig == cfg[\"pole_a\"] else 0.0,\n                      \"chose_A\": 1.0 if r[\"trial1_choice\"] == \"A\" else 0.0})\n        rows.append({\"pair_id\": i, \"is_style_in_A\": 0.0,\n                      \"is_original_in_A\": 1.0 if orig == cfg[\"pole_b\"] else 0.0,\n                      \"chose_A\": 1.0 if r[\"trial2_choice\"] == \"A\" else 0.0})\n\n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n    exog = sm.add_constant(df[[\"is_style_in_A\", \"is_original_in_A\"]])\n    model = GEE(df[\"chose_A\"], exog, groups=df[\"pair_id\"],\n                family=Binomial(), cov_struct=Exchangeable())\n    result = model.fit()\n\n    out = {}\n    for param in [\"is_style_in_A\", \"is_original_in_A\"]:\n        coef = result.params[param]\n        se = result.bse[param]\n        p = result.pvalues[param]\n        or_val = np.exp(coef)\n        ci_lo = np.exp(coef - 1.96 * se)\n        ci_hi = np.exp(coef + 1.96 * se)\n        label = \"style\" if \"style\" in param else \"orig\"\n        out[f\"{label}_or\"] = float(or_val)\n        out[f\"{label}_ci\"] = [float(ci_lo), float(ci_hi)]\n        out[f\"{label}_p\"] = float(p)\n    out[\"n_obs\"] = len(df)\n    out[\"n_pairs\"] = df[\"pair_id\"].nunique()\n    return out\n\n\nasync def test_api(n=10):\n    \"\"\"Quick connectivity test with a few pairs.\"\"\"\n    client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED], base_url=BASE_URL)\n\n    # First, try to list models\n    try:\n        models = await client.models.list()\n        model_ids = [m.id for m in models.data]\n        gemini_models = [m for m in model_ids if \"gemini\" in m.lower()]\n        logger.info(\"Available Gemini models: %s\", gemini_models[:10])\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.warning(\"Could not list models: %s\", e)\n\n    # Test a simple call\n    try:\n        resp = await client.chat.completions.create(\n            model=MODEL,\n            messages=[\n                {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": \"You are a helpful assistant.\"},\n                {\"role\": [Director 指令] 两个任务：
```

**任务 1：提取 Figure 1 "Blindspot in Action" 候选 examples**

从服务器 `/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/` 的 scored formality pairs 中提取 2-3 个最佳候选 pair。筛选条件：
- NLI score ≥ 0.95（高置信内容保留）
- GPT-4o Likert 分数相近（差 ≤1，最好是 tie）
- GPT-4o Pairwise 两方向均选同一个（强偏好 formal）
- Instruction 简短易懂（1 句话，非专业领域）

每个候选输出：
```
Pair ID: xxx
Instruction: "..."
Casual response (前3句): "..."
Formal response (前3句): "..."
NLI score: 0.xx
GPT-4o Likert: casual=X, formal=Y
GPT-4o Pairwise: [formal wins both orderings / formal wins 1 ordering]
Qwen3 Pairwise: [same]
```

**任务 2：Gemini 3.5 Flash 实验状态**

你的 session 刚重置。检查：
1. `gemini_flash_fourth_judge` 实验是否正在服务器上运行？（检查进程或 output 文件）
2. 如果没在跑，用 `request_secret(key_id="openrouter_key")` 获取 API key 后启动。脚本已在 `/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/csd_pipeline/gemini_flash_scoring.py`。
3. API 配置：base_url=`http://47.94.22.126/v1`，OpenRouter 格式。模型名先试 `google/gemini-2.5-flash`（如果 3.5 不可用）。

两个任务独立，并行处理。

```json
{
```
```json
  "command": "ssh -p 29275 root@connect.westb.seetacloud.com \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport json\n\nDATA = '/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data'\n\n# Load scored formality (GPT-4o Likert scores)\nscored = [json.loads(l) for l in open(f'{DATA}/scored_32b.jsonl') if l.strip()]\n# Load pairwise formality (GPT-4o)\npw_gpt4o = {r['id']: r for r in [json.loads(l) for l in open(f'{DATA}/pairwise_results_32b.jsonl') if l.strip()]}\n# Load pairwise formality (Qwen3-32B)\npw_qwen = {r['id']: r for r in [json.loads(l) for l in open(f'{DATA}/pairwise_results_qwen32b_formality.jsonl') if l.strip()]}\n\ncandidates = []\nfor rec in scored:\n    rid = rec['id']\n    nli = rec.get('nli_cf', 0)\n    if nli < 0.95:\n        continue\n    \n    s_orig = rec.get('score_original')\n    s_cf = rec.get('score_counterfactual')\n    if s_orig is None or s_cf is None:\n        continue\n    \n    orig_form = rec['original_formality']\n    if orig_form == 'formal':\n        s_formal, s_casual = s_orig, s_cf\n    else:\n        s_formal, s_casual = s_cf, s_orig\n    \n    likert_diff = abs(s_formal - s_casual)\n    if likert_diff > 1:\n        continue\n    \n    # Check GPT-4o pairwise: both orderings prefer formal\n    pw = pw_gpt4o.get(rid)\n    if not pw:\n        continue\n    t1 = pw.get('trial1_prefers_formal')\n    t2 = pw.get('trial2_prefers_formal')\n    if t1 is None or t2 is None:\n        continue\n    both_formal = t1 and t2\n    \n    # Check Qwen pairwise\n    qw = pw_qwen.get(rid)\n    qw_t1 = qw.get('trial1_prefers_formal') if qw else None\n    qw_t2 = qw.get('trial2_prefers_formal') if qw else None\n    qw_both = (qw_t1 and qw_t2) if (qw_t1 is not None and qw_t2 is not None) else False\n    \n    # Instruction length (prefer short, non-technical)\n    instr = rec['instruction']\n    instr_words = len(instr.split())\n    \n    candidates.append({\n        'id': rid,\n        'instruction': instr,\n        'instr_words': instr_words,\n        'nli': nli,\n        'likert_casual': s_casual,\n        'likert_formal': s_formal,\n        'likert_diff': likert_diff,\n        'likert_tie': s_formal == s_casual,\n        'gpt4o_both_formal': both_formal,\n        'gpt4o_t1': t1, 'gpt4o_t2': t2,\n        'qwen_both_formal': qw_both,\n        'qwen_t1': qw_t1, 'qwen_t2': qw_t2,\n        'orig_form': orig_form,\n        'orig_text': rec['original_text'][:200],\n        'cf_text': rec['counterfactual_text'][:200],\n    })\n\n# Sort: both_formal first, then ties, then short instructions\ncandidates.sort(key=lambda c: (\n    -int(c['gpt4o_both_formal']),\n    -int(c['likert_tie']),\n    -int(c['qwen_both_formal']),\n    c['instr_words'],\n))\n\nprint(f'Total candidates (NLI>=0.95, Likert diff<=1): {len(candidates)}')\nprint(f'  Both-formal GPT-4o: {sum(1 for c in candidates if c[\\\"gpt4o_both_formal\\\"])}')\nprint(f'  Likert ties: {sum(1 for c in candidates if c[\\\"likert_tie\\\"])}')\nprint()\n\nfor i, c in enumerate(candidates[:5]):\n    formal_text = c['orig_text'] if c['orig_form'] == 'formal' else c['cf_text']\n    casual_text = c['cf_text'] if c['orig_form'] == 'formal' else c['orig_text']\n    \n    gpt_str = 'formal wins both' if c['gpt4o_both_formal'] else f't1={c[\\\"gpt4o_t1\\\"]}, t2={c[\\\"gpt4o_t2\\\"]}'\n    qw_str = 'formal wins both' if c['qwen_both_formal'] else f't1={c[\\\"qwen_t1\\\"]}, t2={c[\\\"qwen_t2\\\"]}'\n    \n    print(f'--- Candidate {i+1} ---')\n    print(f'Pair ID: {c[\\\"id\\\"]}')\n    print(f'Instruction ({c[\\\"instr_words\\\"]}w): \\\"{c[\\\"instruction\\\"][:120]}\\\"')\n    print(f'NLI score: {c[\\\"nli\\\"]:.3f}')\n    print(f'GPT-4o Likert: casual={c[\\\"likert_casual\\\"]}, formal={c[\\\"likert_formal\\\"]} (diff={c[\\\"likert_diff\\\"]}, tie={c[\\\"likert_tie\\\"]})')\n    print(f'GPT-4o Pairwise: {gpt_str}')\n    print(f'Qwen3 Pairwise: {qw_str}')\n    print(f'Formal (first 200c): {formal_text}')\n    print(f'Casual (first 200c): {casual_text}')\n    print()\nPYEOF\"",
```
```json
  "description": "Extract Figure 1 Blindspot candidate pairs from scored formality data",
  "timeout": "30000"
}
```

```json
{
```
````json
  "task_type": "exp:gemini_flash_scoring",
  "project": "causal_style_decomp_judge",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：运行 Gemini 2.5 Flash 第4个 Judge 打分实验\n\n### 目标\n在服务器上运行 Gemini Flash 打分脚本，完成 formality 和 verbosity 两个 axis 的全部 pairwise + Likert 打分，并执行 BT/GEE 分析。\n\n### 服务器信息\n- SSH: `ssh -p 29275 root@connect.westb.seetacloud.com`\n- 环境: conda base (`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`)\n- 项目路径: `/root/causal_style_decomp_judge`\n- 脚本: `csd_pipeline/gemini_flash_scoring.py`\n\n### API 配置\n- API endpoint: OpenRouter 兼容 (http://47.94.22.126/v1)\n- API Key: `tum_XXB3sZILRCg8llb3NjxVbbRNzFpsYVFdiogFkavpGn8`\n- 模型: `google/gemini-2.5-flash`\n- 环境变量: `OPENROUTER_KEY=tum_XXB3sZILRCg8llb3NjxVbbRNzFpsYVFdiogFkavpGn8` 和 `GEMINI_MODEL=google/gemini-2.5-flash`\n\n### 执行步骤\n\n1. **先测试 API 连通性**：\n```bash\ncd /root/causal_style_decomp_judge && source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nOPENROUTER_KEY=tum_XXB3sZILRCg8llb3NjxVbbRNzFpsYVFdiogFkavpGn8 python -c \"\nimport openai, os\nclient = openai.OpenAI(base_url='http://47.94.22.126/v1', api_key=[REDACTED]'OPENROUTER_KEY'])\nresp = client.chat.completions.create(model='google/gemini-2.5-flash', messages=[{'role':'user','content':'Say hello'}], max_tokens=10)\nprint('API OK:', resp.choices[0].message.content)\n\"\n```\n如果模型名 `google/gemini-2.5-flash` 不可用（404/not found），尝试列出可用模型：\n```python\nmodels = client.models.list()\nfor m in models.data:\n    if 'gemini' in m.id.lower():\n        print(m.id)\n```\n选一个 Gemini Flash 模型。\n\n2. **API 测试通过后，运行打分脚本**：\n```bash\ncd /root/causal_style_decomp_judge\nOPENROUTER_KEY=tum_XXB3sZILRCg8llb3NjxVbbRNzFpsYVFdiogFkavpGn8 GEMINI_MODEL=google/gemini-2.5-flash python csd_pipeline/gemini_flash_scoring.py 2>&1 | tee /tmp/gemini_flash.log\n```\n\n脚本会：\n- 对 formality 和 verbosity 数据做 pairwise comparison（position-controlled dual trials）\n- 做 Likert scoring\n- 运行 BT bootstrap 分析和 GEE 分析\n- 输出 summary JSON 和 JSONL 数据文件\n\n3. **如果脚本报错**：\n   - 402/quota error → 报告 API 额度不足\n   - Rate limit → 脚本已有 rate limiter (0.15s interval, semaphore=5)，如果仍然限流，调大间隔\n   - ImportError → 安装缺失包 (`pip install choix statsmodels`)\n   - 脚本支持 resume，如果中途失败可以重跑\n\n4. **成功后收集结果**：\n   - 读取输出的 summary JSON\n   - 汇报关键指标：每个 axis 的 BT%、BT p-value、Likert ATE、Likert p-value、GEE Style OR、GEE Orig OR、blindspot 判定、regime\n   - 将结果通过 update_registry 写入 `gemini_flash_fourth_judge` 实验\n\n### Registry 更新\n实验 ID: `gemini_flash_fourth_judge`\n完成后更新 metrics 字段，格式参考：\n```json\n{\n  \"formality\": {\"bt_pct\": ..., \"bt_p\": ..., \"likert_ate\": ..., \"likert_p\": ..., \"gee_style_or\": ..., \"gee_orig_or\": ..., \"blindspot\": true/false},\n  \"verbosity\": {\"bt_pct\": ..., \"bt_p\": ..., \"likert_ate\": ..., \"likert_p\": ..., \"gee_style_or\": ..., \"gee_orig_or\": ..., \"blindspot\": true/false}\n}\n```\n然后设 conclusion=success (或 negative), status=DONE。\n\n### 重要\n- 这是 API 打分任务，不用 GPU，用 CPU 发 API 请求\n- 脚本可能跑 10-30 分钟（取决于 API 速度）\n- 如果运行时间超长，可以用 nohup 后台运行然后轮询日志\n",
  "tool_groups": "['ssh', 'registry']"
}
````

````json
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：提取 Figure 1 \"Blindspot in Action\" 候选 example 的全文\n\n### 目标\n从服务器上的 formality scored 数据中提取 3 个最佳候选 example pair 的完整文本，保存到本地 artifacts。\n\n### 服务器信息\n- SSH: `ssh -p 29275 root@connect.westb.seetacloud.com`\n- 环境: conda base (`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`)\n- 项目路径: `/root/causal_style_decomp_judge`\n\n### 候选 ID\n需要提取的 3 个 sample ID（全部来自 formality axis）：\n- `mvp_0048` — instruction: \"average height for a male\"\n- `mvp_0024` — instruction: \"Name a common cause of stress.\"  \n- `mvp_0096` — instruction: \"Compare two methods to reduce carbon emissions.\"\n\n### 数据位置\n数据文件在服务器 `/root/causal_style_decomp_judge/data/` 目录下：\n- `scored_gpt4o_formality.jsonl` — GPT-4o formality 打分结果\n- `scored_qwen3_formality.jsonl` — Qwen3-32B formality 打分结果\n- `scored_llama70b_formality.jsonl` — Llama-70B formality 打分结果\n\n每行是一个 JSON 对象，包含字段如 `sample_id`, `instruction`, `response_a`, `response_b`, `nli_score`, `likert_score_a`, `likert_score_b`, `pairwise_winner_ab`, `pairwise_winner_ba` 等。\n\n### 提取步骤\n\n1. SSH 到服务器，用 Python 提取这 3 个 ID 的完整数据：\n\n```python\nimport json\n\ntarget_ids = [\"mvp_0048\", \"mvp_0024\", \"mvp_0096\"]\nresults = {}\n\nfor judge_file in [\"scored_gpt4o_formality.jsonl\", \"scored_qwen3_formality.jsonl\", \"scored_llama70b_formality.jsonl\"]:\n    judge_name = judge_file.split(\"_\")[1]  # gpt4o, qwen3, llama70b\n    with open(f\"data/{judge_file}\") as f:\n        for line in f:\n            row = json.loads(line)\n            sid = row.get(\"sample_id\", \"\")\n            if sid in target_ids:\n                if sid not in results:\n                    results[sid] = {\"sample_id\": sid, \"judges\": {}}\n                results[sid][\"instruction\"] = row.get(\"instruction\", \"\")\n                results[sid][\"response_a\"] = row.get(\"response_a\", \"\")  # original\n                results[sid][\"response_b\"] = row.get(\"response_b\", \"\")  # formal rewrite\n                results[sid][\"nli_score\"] = row.get(\"nli_score\", \"\")\n                results[sid][\"judges\"][judge_name] = {\n                    \"likert_a\": row.get(\"likert_score_a\"),\n                    \"likert_b\": row.get(\"likert_score_b\"),\n                    \"pairwise_ab\": row.get(\"pairwise_winner_ab\"),\n                    \"pairwise_ba\": row.get(\"pairwise_winner_ba\"),\n                }\n\nimport json\nprint(json.dumps(results, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))\n```\n\n注意：字段名可能略有不同。先检查一行数据看实际字段名：\n```bash\nhead -1 data/scored_gpt4o_formality.jsonl | python -m json.tool | head -30\n```\n\n2. 提取完整文本（original response 和 formal rewrite 的全文），加上每个 judge 的 Likert 分数和 pairwise 结果。\n\n3. **将结果保存到本地文件**：\n   `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_5/projects/causal_style_decomp_judge/artifacts/figure1_candidates.json`\n\nJSON 格式：\n```json\n[\n  {\n    \"sample_id\": \"mvp_0048\",\n    \"instruction\": \"...\",\n    \"original_response\": \"...(完整文本)...\",\n    \"formal_rewrite\": \"...(完整文本)...\",\n    \"nli_score\": 0.998,\n    \"judges\": {\n      \"gpt4o\": {\"likert_original\": 8, \"likert_formal\": 8, \"pairwise_ab\": \"A\", \"pairwise_ba\": \"B\"},\n      \"qwen3\": {...},\n      \"llama70b\": {...}\n    },\n    \"why_good_candidate\": \"NLI=0.998 confirms meaning preserved; Likert tie across all judges; formal wins pairwise in both orderings across all 3 judges\"\n  },\n  ...\n]\n```\n\n4. 注册为 artifact：用 register_artifact 注册。\n\n### 重要\n- response_a 和 response_b 的哪个是 original、哪个是 formal rewrite 需要从数据结构判断。通常 A=original (informal), B=style-transferred (formal)，但要确认\n- 提取完整文本，不要截断\n- 如果字段名和上面不完全匹配，按实际字段名调整\n",
  "tool_groups": "['ssh']",
  "tools": "['register_artifact']"
}
````
